avaṉ varivu maṟuppiṉum-talivi cuṟṟattār talivaṟku varivu maṟuttavaḻiyum : tōḻi aṟattoṭu niliyāṟ kūṟum.
talivikkuk kūṟuvaṉaṅ koḷka.
		amma vāḻi tōḻi nammūrp
pirintōrp puṇarppavar iruntaṉar kollō
taṇṭuṭik kiyar veṇṭalic citavalar	(kuṟun. 146)
itu tamar varivu maṟupparōveṉak kavaṉṟāṭkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
		nuṇṇēr puruvatta kaṇṇu māṭum
mayirvār muṉki vaḷiyuñ ceṟṟum
kaḷiṟukōṭ piḻitta katañciṟan teḻupuli
yeḻutaru maḻiyiṟ kuḻumum
peruṅka ṉāṭaṉ varuṅko laṉṉāy.	(aiṅkuṟu. 218)
itu, tamar varivu maṟuttuḻi āṟṟāta talivikkut tōḻi, tīyakuṟi nīṅki naṟkuṟi taṉakkucceyyakkaṇṭu kaṭitiṉ varivareṉak kūṟiyatu.
muṉṉili aṟaṉ eṉappaṭutal eṉṟu iruvakip puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuppiṉum1 (aṟaṉeṉappaṭutal iruvakip puritīr muṉṉiliyeṉṟu kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuppiṉum) - aṟaṉeṉṟu collappaṭun taṉmi iruvar kaṇṇuṅkuṟṟantīrnta etirppāṭeṉṟu ceviliyiṭattē kūṟi ak kiḷaviyi naṟṟāyiṭat tuñ celuttiṉum;
eṉṟatu, puṉaṟarupuṇarcciyum, pūttaru puṇarcciyuṅ, kaḷiṟu taru puṇarcciyum pōlvaṉa cevilikkuk kūṟi avaḷ naṟṟāykkuk kūṟutali nikaḻttuvittalām. eṉavē, avaḷ tantikkun taṉṉiyarkkum uṇarttutalum ataṉi mīṇṭu vantu talivikku uṇarttutalum peṟṟām. avvaṟattoṭu nili eḻuvakiya(207) eṉap poruḷiyaluṭ kūṟupa.
utāraṇam : kāmar kaṭumpuṉal kalantemmō ṭāṭuvāḷ (kali. 39) eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcikkaliyuḷ, terumantucāyttār tali eṉap puṉaṟarupuṇarcciyāl tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭuniṟpac cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭuniṟpa avaḷ ēṉiyōrkku aṟattoṭu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.
varivu uṭaṉpaṭṭōṟ kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉum - talivi tamarvarivuṭaṉpaṭat tāṉum varivuṭaṉ paṭṭa talivaṉ variviṭi vittup pirintu nīṭṭittuḻi iṉi nīṭṭikkaṟpāli yalliyeṉak kaṭuñcoṟ kūṟi varivukaṭāval vēṇṭiya iṭattum.
āṅku ataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi uḷappaṭa - aṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭāviyavaḻi av varintukōṭaṉ meyyāyiṉamiyiṉ vatuvi muṭiyumaḷavum āṟṟuvataṟku vaṟpuṟuttuk kūṟutal uḷappaṭa;
taṉmi, meymmi. eṉavē muṉ poymmiyāṉa vaṟpuṟuttulum peṟṟām.
u-m:		neykaṉi kuṟumpūḻ kāya māka
ārpatam peṟuka tōḻi yatti
peruṅkaṉ ṉāṭaṉ varinteṉa vavaṉetir
naṉṟō makaṉē yeṉṟali
ṉaṉṟē pōlu meṉṟurit tōṉē.	(kuṟun. 389)
itu, talivaṉ kuṟṟēvaṉ makaṉāṉ varivumalinta tōḻi talivikkurittatu.
		koṭicci kākku maṭukkaṟ pintiṉi
muntuviḷi peruṅkural koṇṭa manti
kallāk kaṭuvaṉoṭu nalvari yēṟi
aṅki niṟiya ñemiṭik koṇṭutaṉ
tiriyaṇaṟ koṭuṅkavu ṇiṟiya mukki
vāṉpeya ṉaṉinta puṟatta nōṉpiyar
tiyū ṇirukkiyiṟ ṟōṉṟu nāṭaṉ
vantaṉaṉ vāḻi tōḻi yulakaṅ
kayaṅka ṇaṟṟa pitaru kālip
pīḷoṭu tiraṅkiya nelliṟku
naḷḷeṉ yāmattu maḻipoḻin tāṅkē.
	naṟṟiṇi, 22:
itaṉuḷ, tiṉiviḷikālam vatuvik kālamāyiṉum vampamāri iṭiyiṭutaliṉṟi yāṉ kūṟiya varivu poyttaṉarēṉum iṉṟu meyyākavē vantaṉareṉṟāḷ.
		kuṉṟiṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ kuvavumaṇa lēṟik
kaṇṭaṉam varukañ ceṉmō tōḻi
taṇṭā rakalam vaṇṭimir pūtap
paṭumaṇik kalimā kaṭii
neṭunīrc cērppaṉ varūu māṟē.	naṟṟiṇi, 235
itu, talivaṉ varivoṭu varukiṉṟami kāṇkam vammō veṉṟatu.
pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭum - pāṅkiyar palaruḷḷum pāṅkāntaṉmi ciṟappak kūṟiya muppattiraṇṭum; nāleṭṭu meṉa ummi virikka; vakiyum1 ikkūṟṟukkaḷilē vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavum; tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ tōḻi mēṉa - poṟuttaṟkariya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya tōḻiyiṭattaṉa (e - ṟu).
eṉavē, oṉṟiya tōḻikkaṉṟi ēṉiyōrkku ikkūṟṟu iṉṟeṉṟāṉ, tāyttāyk koṇṭuvaruñ ciṟappum,2 iruvar tuṉpamun tāṉ uṟṟāḷākak karutuñ ciṟappum uṭimiyiṉ tāṅkaruñ ciṟappu eṉṟāṉ. urittuḻik kūṭṭattōṭē akaṟciyum eṉṟalum ūṭṭalum urittalum accuṟuttalum eñcāmaṟ kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷaviyum pāṅkuṟa vanta eṉka. nāṭṭattiṉ kaṇṇum eñcāmaṟ kiḷanta eṉka. eṉṟatu, ārāycciyuṭaṉē ivveṭṭuṅ kūṟumeṉṟāṉ. peyarppiṉum oḻippiṉum urittuḻik kūṭṭattōṭu eñcāmaṟ kiḷanta eṉka. ēṉipporuḷkaḷ ēḻaṉurupum viṉiyeccamumāy niṉṟavaṟṟip pāṅkuṟa vanta eṉpataṉōṭu muṭittu appeyareccattiṉi nāleṭṭeṉṉum peyarōṭu muṭittu aṉit tōḻimēṉaveṉa muṭikka.
iṉi vaki yāṟ koḷvaṉa varuvāṟu:
		aṉṉi vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi neytal
nīrppaṭar tūmpiṉ pūkkeḻu tuṟivaṉ
entōḷ tuṟanta kāli yevaṉkel
paṉṉāḷ varumava ṉaḷitta poḻutē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 109)
iḵtu aṟattoṭu niṉṟapiṉ varivāṉ pirintu nīṭṭittuḻi aiyuṟṟa cevili avaṉ nummit tuṟantāṉ pōlum nuṅkaṭku avaṉ kūṟiyatiṟam yāteṉṟāṭkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
		aṉṉi vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi kaḻaṉiya
muṇṭaka malarun taṇkaṭaṟ cērppaṉ
entōḷ tuṟantaṉa ṉāyiṉ
evaṉkoṉ maṟṟava yinta tōḷē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 108)
iḵtu aṟattoṭu niṉṟapiṉ varivunīṭa maṟṟoru kula makaḷi variyuṅkoleṉṟu aiyuṟṟa cevili kuṟippaṟinta tōḻi avaṭkuk kūṟiyatu.
		aṉṉi vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi puṉṉiyōṭu
ñāḻal pūkkun taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ
ivaṭkamin taṉaṉāṟ ṟāṉē
taṉakkamin taṉṟivaṇ māmik kaviṉē.
	(aiṅkuṟu. 103)
itu. vatuvi nikaḻāniṉṟuḻit tāykkuk kāṭṭit tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
		kaṉṉavi ṟōḷāṉ kaṭināḷ vilakkutaṟ
keṉṉi poruṇiṉintā rēntiḻāy---piṉṉar
emarēṟṟuk koḷḷumeṉ ṟañciṉē ṉañcār
namarēṟṟuk koḷḷāta ñāṉṟu.
itu, cuṟṟattār poruḷvēṇṭi maṟuttāreṉṟatu.
		notuma lāḷar koḷḷā riviyē
yemmoṭu vantu kaṭalāṭu makaḷirum
neytalam pakittaḻip pāvi puṉiyār
uṭalakaṅ koḷvō riṉmiyiṟ
ṟoṭalik kuṟṟa cilapū viṉarē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 187)
itu, kiyuṟi maṟuttatu.
		amma vāḻi tōḻi nammali
variyā miḻiyak kōṭa ṉīṭak
kātalarp pirintōr kiyaṟa naliyun
taṇpaṉi vaṭanti yacciram
muntuvan taṉarnaṅ kāta lōrē.	(aiṅkuṟu 223)
itu, variviṭip pirintōṉ kuṟitta paruvattiṟkumuṉ varukiṉṟami yaṟinta tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟiyatu	(24)
āyvuri
kaḷavoḻukkattil tōḻi uriyāṭutaṟkuriya poruḷ vaki yellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttuk kūṟuvatu innūṟpāvākum.
(i-ḷ) naṟumaṇam, tōṟṟappolivu, oḻukkam, uṇavu, ceyviṉi maṟittal, celavu, payilvu eṉat talivi talivaṉi etirppaṭutaṟku muntuṟṟa nilimiyi maṉattuḷ koṇṭuvarum eḻuvakiyuṇarvukaḷālum talivikkuk kūṭṭamuṇmi yaṟinta piṉṉar, meymmiyākavum poymmiyākavum vaḻipāṭṭu niliyiṟpiḻiyātu palavāki vēṟupaṭum poruḷkaḷiyuṭiya coṟkaḷik kūṟi ārāytaṟkaṇṇum, taṉpāṟkuṟiyirattaṟ poruṭṭu etirppaṭṭa talimakaṉip piṟaraṟiyāvāṟu avaṉatu perumiyiṉi eṭutturittu avviṭattiviṭṭu nīṅkumāṟu ceytaṟkaṇṇum, ulakattār maṇañceytukoḷḷumāṟu varintukoḷ eṉak kūṟumiṭattum, taliviyi aṇukutaṟku iyalāmiyik kūṟi akaṟṟumiṭattum, niṉṉāṟ kātalikkap paṭṭāḷukku niṉ kuṟiyiṉi nīyē yaṟivuṟutti ataṉpiṉ eṉṉiṭam varuka eṉak kūṟutalum, niṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa avaḷ niṉ kātaliyuṇarum mutuk kuṟivu peṟāta pētip paruvattiṉaḷ eṉak kūṟutalum, muṉṉar avaḷik kūṭiyavāṟē avaḷiyaṭika eṉak kūṟi muṟimippaṭa oḻukumāṟu aṟivuṟutti niṟuttalum, ikkaḷavoḻukkam tanti taṉṉiyarkkup pulaṉāyiṉ yātāy viḷiyumō eṉa añciyavāḷay avvaccattiṉit talivaṉukku aṟivuṟuttalum, taṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭāḷ iṉṉa taṉmiyaḷ eṉat talivaṉ kūṟiyavaḻi avaḷum niṉ taṉmiyaḷē eṉa avaḷōṭu avaṉi oppumi tōṉṟac cērtturittalum eṉa oḻiyātu kūṟappaṭṭa eṇvakik kūṟṟukkaḷum, poyyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷip puṉintu kūṟikkoṇṭu taṉpāṟ kuṟiyirantu niṉṟa talimakaṉi(t talimakaḷ ikaḻātu) poṟuttakāraṇamākiya iruvaratu pēraṉpiṉi eṇṇiyakālattum, mēṟcollappaṭṭa mūvakiyāṉum talimakaḷoṭu talivaṉukkut kūṭṭa muṇmi yuṇarntapiṉ talivaṉpāṟ paṇivuṭaṉ oḻukumiṭattum, talimakaṉpāṟ kuṟinērntāḷ avaṉatu kuṟinayappittal vēṇṭit talimakaḷpāṟ cellutaṟkaṇṇum, tāṉ kuṟippāl uṇarttum kuṟinayappiṟkut talivamakaḷ nāṇmikutiyāṟ karantavuḷḷattiṉaḷāy uṭampaṭātāḷ pōṉṟu otuṅkiya niliyil taṉṉōṭum avaḷōṭum kuṟippiṉāṟ poruntiya ceytikaḷik kaṭṭik kūṟi vaḻipaṭṭumuyalum palvēṟu pakutikkaṇṇum, talivi kuṟinayantami peṟṟavaḻi ataṉit talimakaṉukku uṇarttu miṭattum, eṇṇutaṟku ariya palavākiya nakikkuṟippuṭiya uriyāṭalkaḷik karutiya pakutiyilum, talivaṉ taliviyuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvutali virumpiyaviṭattum, taṉṉuḷḷam virumpāta piriviṉi vēṇṭiya kālattum, talivaṉi viruntāka ēṟṟup pōṟṟuvataṉit talivi virumpiyakālattum, talivaṉoṭu talivi kūṭiyavaḻi ataṉiyaṟiyātāḷ pōṉṟu paḻakum aṟivu maṭampaṭṭa ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇum, taliviyit talivaṉpāṟ pātukāppuk kūṟikkoṭukkum naṭpiṉ pakutiyilum, taliviyiṉpāṟ pēraṉpuṭiya talivaṉik kuṟittu(t taṉ maṉattakattē naṉṟāṟ-ṟalākiya) cemmiyum (avaṉ taliviyi virivil maṇantu koḷḷāmi kuṟittup puṟattē) kaṭumiyum vāynta coṟkaḷāl iyaṟpaḻittavaḻit taliviyiṉ neñcam citivuṟṟu varuntiya niliyilum, eṉpum urukumāṟu talivaṉatu pirivaṉāl varuntum taliviyiṉ pāṟceṉṟu tāṉ kūṟuvataṉi avaḷatu uḷḷam ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi celuttit talivaṉatu aṉpu talimakaḷiṭattatāka vaṟpuṟuttumiṭattum,talivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṉatu tīmiyit tāṅkaḷ aṟitaluṟṟamiyāl nērnta kalakkattiṉ kaṇṇum, talimakaḷi iṟceṟittuk kāttaliṉkaṭumi ellikaṭantami yālum, pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi eṉṉum iṭamum pakalum iravumākiya poḻutum ākattāṅkaḷ varintu kūṟum nilimiyi vilakki, talivaṉ taliviyiruvariṭiyē yaminta kātal mikutiyuṭpaṭat talimakaṉukkuriya nāṭu, ūr, maṉi, kuṭiyuyarvu, piṟantamāṇṭu, peṟṟuḷḷa ciṟappu eṉpaṉa piṟariṉ ovvātu mēmpaṭṭaṉavātal nōkkit talivaṉik kuṟittut tōṉṟiya kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi attaṉmittākiya nilimiyiṉkūṟupāṭṭiṉālēmaṇañ ceytali vēṇṭiyum, talivaṉoruvaṉōṭu talivikkuk kūṭṭam nērntirukkumō eṉa aiyuṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya nikaḻcciyik kaṇṇuṟṟu viṉaviya ceviliyākiya tāykku etir niṉṟu acceytiyippoyyeṉa maṟuttu meymmiyāṉa neṟiyil nikaḻtaṟkuriya vēṟōr nikaḻcciyāka avaḷ nampumpaṭi paṭittuk kāṭṭiyum, talivi melintu vēṟupaṭṭa kālattu avaḷatu melivutīra aṟivari viṉāvikkaṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭuk kuṟi pārkkumiṭattum, veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttumiṭattum ayalā ṉoruvaṉukkut taliviyi maṇañceytu koṭuttalic cuṟṟattār maṟuttaniliyilum, muṉṉiliyākki moḻital aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉṉum iruvakiyiṉālum kuṟṟantīrnta coṟkaḷic cevilikkuk kūṟi ataṉi naṟṟāy ceviyiṟ pukumpaṭi ceytaṟkaṇṇum, taliviyiṉ cuṟṟattār makaṭ koṭuppatāka uṭaṉpaṭṭa talivaṉi virintu maṇantu koḷka eṉat tūṇṭutaṟ kaṇṇum, ivvāṟu varivuṭampaṭṭa taṉmiyiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟit taliviyi āṟṟiyirukkum vaṇṇam vaṟpuṟuttaṟ kaṇṇum eṉat taliviyuṭaṉ taṉakkuṟṟatākiya pāṅkāntaṉmi ciṟappuṟa vanta muppattiraṇṭu vakip poruṇmiyum piṟarevarālum mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkariya peruñciṟappiṉiyuṭiya tōḻiyiṭattaṉa e---ṟu.
innūṟpāviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa muppattiraṇṭu poruṇmi- kaḷāvaṉa :
1.	nāṟṟa mutaliya uṇarcciyēḻiṉum uṇarntapiṉ meyyiṉum 	poyyiṉum vaḻinilipiṟaḻātu palvēṟu kavarporuḷ 	nāṭṭam, itu pāṅki matiyuṭaṉpāṭṭil muṉṉuṟavuṇar 	taliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
2.	kuṟiyuṟaṟku etirppaṭṭa talivaṉip perumiyiṟ peyarttal.
3.	ulakurittu oḻittal.
4.	arumiyiṉ akaṟṟal.
5.	alar aṟivuṟuttup piṉvā eṉṟal.
6.	pētimiyūṭṭal.
7.	muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟi niṟuttu urittal.
8.	añci accuṟuttal.
9.	urittuḻik kūṭṭam. ivi eṭṭum kuṟiyuṟavuṇartaliṉ 	pāṟpaṭum.
10.	vanta kiḻavaṉi māyañ ceppip poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟittal: 	iḵtu iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ varavuṇartaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
11.	puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkal.
12.	kuṟintu avaṭpaṭartal: aḵtāvatu kuṟinayappac  cēṟal.
13.	taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum muṉṉam muṉtaḷiip piṉṉili 	nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅku: aḵtāvatu kuṟinayappuvaki.
14.	naṉṉayampeṟṟuḻi nayampuri iṭam: aḵtāvatu talimakaḷ 	kuṟi nayantami talivaṉukkuk kūṟal.
15.	eṇṇarum palnaki kaṇṇiya vaki: aḵtāvatu alarāku 	meṉṟu kūṟutal.
16.	talivaṉ puṇarcci vēṇṭiya nili.
17.	puṇarcci vēṇṭātu pirivu vēṇṭiya nili.
18.	vēḷāṇperuneṟi vēṇṭiya nili.
19.	puṇarntuḻiyuṇarnta aṟimaṭac ciṟappu: aḵtāvatu alla- 	kuṟippaṭutal.
20.	ōmpaṭik kiḷavi.
21.	ceṅkaṭumoḻiyāl aṉpu taliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟi: aḵtāvatu 	talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittut talimakaḷi vaṟpuṟuttal.
22.	āṟiṉṉāmi aṟivuṟukalakkam: atāvatu talivaṉ varum vaḻi 		palvēṟu tīmikaḷiyuṭiyatu eṉṟal.
23.	kāppiṉmikuti kiyaṟavurittal: aḵtāvatu talimakaḷip 	peṟṟōr iṟceṟittuk kākkum kāvalmikutiyiṉik kūṟutal.
24.	kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakkik kātal mikuti kūṟal.
25.	nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum piṟappum ciṟappum iṟappa nōkki 	avaṉ vayiṉ tōṉṟiya kiḷaviyoṭu tokii aṉinili 	vakiyāṉ varital vēṇṭutal.
26.	aiyacceyki tāykku etirmaṟuttup poy eṉa māṟṟi mey 	vaḻikkoṭuttal.
27.	avaḷ vilaṅkuṟutal: aḵtāvatu talivi melintu vēṟupaṭṭu 	varuntum niliyil ammelivukkuriya kāraṇam yātu eṉa 	ārāyntu aṟiyum poruṭṭuk kaṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭuk 	kuṟipārkkum niliyil avaḷ poruṭṭuk kuṟipārttali 	vilakkutal.
28.	kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭutal : talimakaḷatu nōy taṇittal vēṇṭi 	vēlaṉik koṇṭu veṟiyāṭap pukkuḻi av veṟiyiṉi 		vilakkal.
29.	piṟaṉ varivu āytal: aḵtāvatu talimakaḷip piṟaṉoru- 	vaṉukku maṇam ceytu koṭuttalip peṟṟōr ārāyu 	miṭattup piṟaṉ varivu maṟuppittal.
30.	avaṉ varivu maṟuttal: aḵtāvatu talivaṉukku maṇañ-	ceytu koṭuttalic cuṟṟattār maṟutta niliyil tali makaṉukkut talimakaḷi maṇañceytu koṭuttaṟkup peṟṟōri uṭaṉpaṭac ceytal. mēṟkuṟitta nāṉkiṭaṅ kaḷilum muṉṉili, aṟaṉeṉappaṭutal eṉṟu iruvakik kuṟṟan tīrnta moḻikaḷāṟ ceviliyākiya tāy vaḻiyāka naṟṟāy ceviyiṟ pukumāṟu tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ.
31.	varivuṭampaṭṭōṟ kaṭāval vēṇṭutal : aḵtāvatu tam cuṟṟattār varivukku uṭaṉpaṭṭa ceytiyit talivaṉukku uṇarttal.
32.	āṅku ataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi: avvāṟu tāṉ virumpiya talivaṉukkuc cuṟṟattār varivuṭampaṭṭami kūṟit taliviyi varuttam taṇika eṉa vaṟpuṟutti āṟṟuvittal.
	mēṟkuṟitta muppattiraṇṭu poruḷ vakipaṟṟiyum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻttaṟku uriyaḷ eṉpatām.
25.	kaḷavala rāyiṉuṅ kāmamēṟ paṭuppiṉum
aḷavumikat tōṉṟiṉun talippeytu kāṇiṉuṅ
kaṭṭiṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa iruvarum
oṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytik kaṇṇum
āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi aḻivutali variṉum
kātal kimmikak kaṉaviṉ araṟṟalun
tōḻiyi viṉātalun teyvam vāḻttalum
pōkkuṭaṉ aṟintapiṉ tōḻiyoṭu keḻīik
kaṟpiṉ ākkattu niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum
piriviṉ eccattu makaḷneñcu valippiṉum
irupāṟ kuṭipporuḷ iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum
iṉṉa vakiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyoṭu
aṉṉavi piṟavuñ cevili mēṉa.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu mēṟ ṟalivaṟkun talivikkun tōḻikkumuriya kiḷavi-yellāṅ kūṟi iṉic cevilikkuriya kiḷavi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
kaḷavalarātal mutalākac collappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyum attaṉmiya piṟa kiḷaviyum kaḷavukkālattuc ceviliyiṉ mēlaṉ eṉṟavāṟu. ivaṟṟuḷ tōḻiyi viṉātaleṉa vēṟoru kiḷaviyāka ōtiṉārāyiṉum ataṉ muṉpu nikaḻum kiḷaviyellām avaḷi viṉātaṟkuk kāraṇamātaliṉ avi yīṇṭup patiṉ mūṉṟeṉa veṇṇappaṭṭaṉa veṉka.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavala rāyiṉum eṉpatu - talivaṉ oḻukalāṟu puṟattārkkup pulaṉāki alar tūṟṟappaṭṭa viṭattut tōḻiyi viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
kāma mēṟ paṭuppiṉum eṉpatu --- talivimāṭṭuḷatākiya vēṭki aḷaviṟappiṉum tōḻiyi viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
		maṇiyil tikaḻtaru nūlpōl maṭanti
aṇiyil tikaḻvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.	(kuṟaḷ. 1273)
aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum eṉpatu --- petumpip paruvat taḷākiya talivi puṇarcciyāṟ katirttu vīṅkukiṉṟa muliyum putituṟṟa kaliṉuṅ kaṇṭaviṭattun tōḻiyi viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
		kaṇ niṟinta kārikik kāmpērtōṭ pētikkup
peṇ niṟinta nīrmi peritu	(kuṟaḷ. 1272)
talippeytu kāṇiṉumeṉpatu talivaṉoṭu talivi-viyit talippeytu kāṇiṉum viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
peyteṉpataṉip peyaveṉat tirikka.
	.......................................
miṭiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ
eṉa varum.
kaṭṭiṉum eṉpatu --- kaṭṭu vippittavaḻiyum avarcoṟ-kēṭṭut tōḻiyi viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
kaḻaṅkiṉum eṉpatu --- kaḻaṅku vittuḻiyum avarcoṟ-kēṭṭut tōḻiyi viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
veṟiyeṉa viruvaru moṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytikkaṇṇum eṉpatu-ceviliyum naṟṟāyum poruntiyapakkattuk kaṇṭu veṟiyāṭuvā meṉṟavaḻit talivi ceytikkaṇṇun tōḻiyi viṉāvum eṉṟavāṟu.
āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi aḻivu talivariṉum eṉpatu --- veṟiyāṭiya ceṉṟavaḻi ataṟkaḻivuṟumiṭattu variṉum eṉṟavāṟu.
aḵtāvatu: kaṭavuṭ kaṟcuṉi eṉat toṭaṅkum naṟṟiṇip pāṭṭil,
		niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmi aṟintum aṇṇāntu
kārnaṟuṅ kaṭampiṉ kaṇṇi cūṭi
vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉi vantōy
kaṭavu ḷāyiṉum āka
maṭavi maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē	(naṟṟiṇi, 34)
eṉat tōḻi kūṟutal. avvāṟukūṟiyavaḻiyuṅ kāraṇa meṉṉi yeṉaviṉāvum.
kātal kimmikak kaṉavi ṉaraṟṟalum eṉpatu --- kātaṉ mikutiyāl talivaṉi yuḷḷik kaṉaviṉkaṇ araṟṟutaṟkaṇṇum viṉāvum
tōḻiyi viṉātalum eṉpatu---ivi nimittamākat tōḻiyi viṉātalum eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē kaḷavalarātal. mutaṟ kaṉaviṉaraṟṟa līṟāka ōtiya voṉpatu kiḷaviyum tōḻiyi viṉātaṟpakuti. avi nikaḻātavaḻi viṉātalilli. ataṉāl tōḻiyi viṉātaleṉa oru kiḷaviyāka eṇṇaṟka.
teyvam vāḻttalum eṉpatu --- ivvāṟu paṭṭateṉat tōḻi yurittavaḻi yitaṉi naṟṟāykkum tantikkum kūṟalāṟṟā- tāḷ teyvatti vēṇṭikkōṭal.
pōkkuṭaṉ aṟintapiṉ... niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum eṉpatu--- talivaṉuṭaṉ pōyiṉāḷ eṉṟu aṟintavaḻit tāṉun tōḻi yōṭu keḻumi illattiṉkaṇiṟuttaṟ kaṇṇum eṉṟavāṟu.
		paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa iṟikoḷpu
toṉmū tālattup potiyil tōṉṟiya
nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla
vāyā kiṉṟē tōḻi āykaḻaṟ
cēyili veḷvēl viṭaliyoṭu
tokuvaḷi muṉki maṭanti naṭpē.	(kuṟun. 15)
piriviṉeccattu makaṇeñcu valippiṉum eṉpatu --- talivaṉ variyātu pirintavaḻi yoḻinta talimakaḷ alarākutalu miṉṟi vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi oru maṉippaṭṭirunta vuḷḷakkarutti yaṟinta vaḻiyum eṉṟavāṟu. valittal eṉpatu teḷital.
irupāṟ kuṭipporu ḷiyalpiṉkaṇṇum eṉpatu---talivaṉ kuṭimi taṉ kuṭimiyō ṭokkumeṉa vārāytaṟ kaṇṇum eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṭiyēṉṉātu poruḷ eṉṟataṉāl poruḷuṅ kuṇamum āyappeṟu meṉṟavāṟu.
aṉṉavi piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ,
		nāṟṟam peṟṟu nilippuk kāṇṭal
uṇṭiyiṟ kuṟital uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal
kaṇtuyil maṟuttal kōlañcey yāmi
mutalāyiṉa koḷka. iviyum viṉātaṟkētuvām. ivaṟṟiṟ kellāñ ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka.	(25)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu, cevilikūṟṟu1 nikaḻumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. akkūṟṟuc cevili tāṉē kūṟappaṭuvaṉavun, taliviyun tōḻiyuṅ koṇṭu kūṟṟākak kūṟappaṭuvaṉavumeṉa iruvakiyavām ; ikkūṟappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyum avipōlvaṉa piṟavuñ cevili tāṉē kūṟappaṭuvaṉavun taliviyun tōḻiyum avaḷ kūṟṟāyk koṇ ṭeṭuttu moḻiyappaṭuvaṉavumāyc cevilik kuriyavāmeṉṟavāṟu. iṉṉavaki yeṉṟār, taṉ kūṟṟuṅ koṇṭu kūṟṟumāy nikaḻumeṉṟaṟku.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavu alar āyiṉum-kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāy alar tūṟṟappaṭiṉum :
u-m:		pāvaṭi yurala pakuvāy vaḷḷi
ētiṉ mākka ṇuvaṟalu nuvalpa
aḻiva tevaṉkolip pēti yūrkkē
perumpūṭ poṟiyaṉ pēeḻutir kollik
karuṅkaṭ ṭeyvaṅ kuṭavari yeḻutiya
nalliyaṟ pāvi yaṉṉaveṉ
melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pāṭiṉaḷ kuṟiṉē.	(kuṟun. 89)
itu cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu.
		amma vāḻi tōḻi neṉṉal
ōṅkutiri veṇmaṇa luṭikkun tuṟivaṟ
kūrār peṇṭeṉa moḻiya veṉṉi
yatukēṭ ṭaṉṉā yeṉṟaṉa ḷaṉṉi
pipiya vemmi yeṉṟaṉeṉ yāṉē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 113)
itaṉuḷ, peṇṭeṉṟataṉik kēṭṭu aṉṉāyeṉṟaṉaḷ aṉṉiyeṉa alar tūṟṟiṉami kaṇṭu cevili kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉit talivi koṇṭukūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
kāmam meyppaṭuppiṉum-talivi karantoḻukuṅ kāman tāṉē ak kaḷaviṉi naṉṟāyun tītāyum meykkaṇ veḷippaṭup piṉum :
u-m:		maṇiyiṟ ṟikaḻtaru nūlpōṉ maṭanti
yaṇiyiṟ ṟikaḻvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.	(kuṟaḷ, 1273)
itu, kāmattāl tikaḻnta poliviṉic cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu.
aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum-kaṇṇun tōḷum muliyum piṟavum puṇarcciyāṟ katirttuk kāriki nīravāy avaḷiṭattu aḷavi mikakkāṭṭiṉum :
u-m:		kaṇṇiṟinta kārikik kāmpērtōṭ pētikkup
peṇṇiṟinta nīrmi peritu	(kuṟaḷ. 1272)
itu, katirppukkaṇṭu cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu.
		piṉṉuviṭa neṟitta kūntalum poṉṉeṉa
ākat tarumpiya cuṇaṅkum vampuviṭak
kaṇṇurut teḻutaru muliyu nōkki
yellivaḷ periteṉap paṉmāṇ kūṟip
peruntō ḷaṭiya muyaṅki nīṭuniṉin

aliyal vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi
eṉṟatu, talivi puṟattuppōkak kaṇṭu cevili kūṟiyataṉit tōḻi koṇṭukūṟiṉāḷ. tāṉē kūṟuvaṉa vantuḻik kāṇka.
kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa iruvarum oṭṭiya tiṟat ceytikkaṇṇum1 kaṭṭuvicciyum vēlaṉun tām pārtta kaṭṭiṉāṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉāṉun teyvattiṟkuc ciṟappuc ceyyāk kāl im miyal tīrāteṉṟu kūṟutaliṉ, avviruvarun tammiṉotta tiṟampaṟṟiyataṉiyē ceyyuñ ceytiyiṭattum :
tiṟam eṉṟataṉāṉ avar vēṟu vēṟākavuṅ kūṟappaṭum.
u-m:		peymmaṇaṉ muṟṟaṅ kaviṉpeṟa iyaṟṟi
malivāṉ kōṭṭa ciṉiiya vēlaṉ
kaḻaṅkiṉā ṉaṟikuva teṉṟāl
naṉṟā lamma niṉṟaviva ṇalaṉē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 248)
itu vēlaṉ kaḻaṅku pārttami kūṟiṟṟu.
		aṟiyā miyiṉ veṟiyeṉa mayaṅki
aṉṉiyu maruntuya ruḻantaṉaḷ ataṉāl
eyyātu viṭutalō koṭitē niriyitaḻ
āymala ruṇkaṇ pacappac
cēymali nāṭaṉ ceyta nōyē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 242)
itu veṟiyeṉa aṉṉi mayaṅkiṉami kūṟiṟṟu.
aṇaṅkuṭi neṭuvari eṉṉum (22) akappāṭṭiṉuṭ kaṭṭukkaṇṭu veṟiyeṭuttami kūṟiṟṟu. paṉivari nivanta eṉṉum (98) akappāṭṭiṉuṭ pirappuḷar pirīi eṉak kaṭṭu vicciyik kēṭṭavāṟum. eṉ makaṭku eṉac cevili kūṟṟu nikaḻntavāṟuṅ kāṇka. itaṉuḷ neṭuvē ṇalkuvaṉeṉiṉē eṉat talivi añcavēṇṭiyatu. iruvarum oṭṭik kūṟāmal teyvantāṉ aruḷumeṉṟu kōṭaliṉ.
		ikuḷi kēṭṭiciṉ kātalan tōḻi
kuvaḷi yuṇkaṇ ṭēṉpaṉi malka
vaṟitiyāṉ varuntiya cellaṟ kaṉṉi
piṟitoṉṟu kaṭuttaṉa ḷāki vēmpiṉ
veṟikoḷ pācili nilamoṭu cūṭi
yuṭalunark kaṭanta kaṭalan tāṉit
tiruntili neṭuvēṟ ṟeṉṉavaṉ potiyil
aruñcimi yiḻitaru mārttuvara laruviyiṟ
ṟatumpucī riṉṉiyaṅ kaṟaṅkak kitoḻu
turukeḻu ciṟappiṉ muruku maṉittarīik
kaṭampuṅ kaḷiṟum pāṭi nuṭaṅkupu
tōṭun toṭaliyuṅ kikkoṇ ṭalkalum
āṭiṉa rātal naṉṟō nīṭu
niṉṉoṭu teḷitta naṉmali nāṭaṉ
kuṟivara larināṭ kuṉṟat tucci
neṟikeṭa vīḻnta tuṉṉaruṅ kūriruḷ
tirumaṇi yumiḻnta nākaṅ kāntaḷ
koḻumaṭaṟ putuppū vūtun tumpi
naṉṉiṟa maruḷu maruviṭar
iṉṉā nīḷiṭi niṉiyumē ṉeñcē	(akam. 138)
eṉṉum maṇimiṭipavaḷam vitantu kūṟāmiyiṉ iraṇṭum1 oruṅku vantatu.
āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi aḻivu talivariṉum - aṅṅaṉam veṟi yāṭutal vēṇṭiya toḻiṉ muṭinta piṉṉun talivikku varuttam mikiṉum :
u-m:		vēṅki yirumpuṉattu vīḻuṅ kiḷikaṭiyāḷ
kāntaṇ mukiḻviralāṟ kaṇṇiyuṅ kitoṭāḷ
ēnteḻi lalkuṟ ṟaḻipuṉiyā ḷellēyeṉ
pūntoṭi yiṭṭa pulampu maṟitirō
eṉavum
		puṉiyiruṅ kuvaḷip pōtuviri nāṟṟañ
cuṉiyara makaḷi ravvē ciṉiya
vēṅki yoḷvī veṟikamaḻ nāṟṟamoṭu
kānta ṇāṟupa kallara makaḷir
akilu māramu nāa ṟupavaṉ
tiṟalaru marapiṟ ṟeyva meṉpa
veṟipuṉaṅ kāva liruntataṟ ṟoṭṭut
tīviya nāṟu meṉmakaḷ
aṟiyēṉ yāṉiḵ tañcutaka vuṭittē
eṉavum varum.
ivi āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu añcic cevili piṟari viṉāyiṉa.
		aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi niṉmakaḷ
pālu muṇṇāḷ paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭu
naṉipacan taṉaḷeṉa viṉavuti ataṉṟiṟam
yāṉun teṟṟeṉa vuṇarēṉ mēṉāṇ
malipūñ cāraleṉ ṟōḻi mārō
ṭoliciṉi vēṅki koykuvañ ceṉṟuḻip
pulipuli yeṉṉum pūca ṟoṉṟa
oṇceṅ kaḻunīrk kaṇpō lāyitaḻ
ūci pōkiya cūḻcey māliyaṉ
pakkañ cērttiya ceccik kaṇṇiyaṉ
kuyamaṇ ṭākañ ceñcāntu nīvi
varipuṉi villaṉ orukaṇi terintukoṇ
ṭiyātō maṟṟam mātiṟam paṭareṉa
viṉavi niṟṟan tōṉē avaṟkaṇ
ṭemmu ḷemmuṇ meymmaṟi poṭuṅki
nāṇi niṉṟaṉa mākap pēṇi
aivaki vakutta kūnta lāynutaṉ
miyī rōti maṭavīr numvāyp
poyyu muḷavō veṉṟaṉaṉ piyeṉap
parimuṭuku tavirtta tēra ṉetirmaṟuttu
niṉmaka ḷuṇkaṇ paṉmā ṇōkkic
ceṉṟōṉ maṉṟavak kuṉṟukiḻa vōṉē
pakaṉmāy antip paṭucuṭa ramayat
tavaṉmaṟi tēe nōkki maṟṟivaṉ
makaṉē tōḻi yeṉṟaṉaḷ
ataṉaḷa vuṇṭukōḷ matival lōrkkē.	(akam.48)
itu, cevilikūṟṟiṉit tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiyatu.
kātal kimikak kaṉaviṉ araṟṟalum - taliviyiṭattuk kātal kiyikantu perukutalāl tuyilā niṉṟuḻiyum oṉṟu kūṟiaraṟṟutalum :
kaṉavu-tuyil, tuyiliṟ kāṇṭalik kaṉaviṟ kāṇṭa leṉpa.
utāraṇam : poḻutu melliṉṟu (kuṟun. 161) eṉpataṉuṭ putalvaṟ pulli aṉṉā eṉṟu taliviyi viḷittatu kaḷaviṉ araṟṟalāyiṟṟu.
araṟṟal, iṉṉatōr iṉṉākkālattu eṉ ceykiṉṟā yeṉak kātalpaṟṟi iraṅkutal.
tōḻiyi viṉavalum-niṉṟōḻikku iv vēṟupāṭu eṟṟiṉā ṉāyiṟ ṟeṉṟāṟpōlat tōḻiyi viṉāvutalum:
u-m:		neṭuvē lēnti nīyemak kiyāar
toṭuta lōmpeṉa varaṟṟalu maraṟṟum
kaṭavuḷ vēṅkiyuṅ kāntaḷu malinta
toṭalik kaṇṇi pariyalu meṉṉum
pāmpupaṭa nivanta payamaḻit taṭakkip
pūmpoṟik kaḻaṟkā lāay kuṉṟattuk
kuṟuñcuṉi malarnta kuvaḷi nāṟic
ciṟutēṉ kamaḻnta vammel lākam
vāḻiyem makaḷi yurimati immalit
tēmpoti kiḷaviyeṉ pēti
yāṅkā ṭiṉaḷō niṉṉoṭu pakalē
eṉavarum.
itu cevili tōḻiyi viṉāyatu.
		ōṅkumali nāṭa voḻikaniṉ vāymi
kāmputali maṇanta kallatarc ciṟuneṟi
uṟupaki pēṇā tiraviṉ vantivaḷ
poṟikiḷa rākam pullat tōḷcēr
paṟukāṟ paṟavi yaḷavila moyttaliṉ
kaṇkō ḷāka nōkkip paṇṭu
miṉiyi yōveṉa viṉaviṉaḷ yāyē
yataṉetir collā ḷāki allān
teṉmuka nōkki yōḷē yaṉṉā
yāṅkuṇarn tuykuvaḷ kolleṉa maṭutta
cānta ñekiḻi kāṭṭi
yīṅkā yiṉavā leṉṟiciṉ yāṉē.	(naṟṟiṇi. 55)
itu, cevili viṉāyiṉamiyit tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiṉāḷ.
teyvam vāḻttalum - iṉṉatoṉṟu nikaḻntateṉat tuṇinta piṉṉart taṉ makaḷōṭu talimakaṉiṭi nikaḻnta oḻukkam naṉṉarttākaveṉat teyvattiṟkup parāvutalum:
		nāṭaṟkiṉṟīm palavi ṉērkeḻu celvat
tentiyu metirntaṉaṉ koṭiyē yalarvāy
ampa lūru mavaṉoṭu moḻiyum
cāyiṟit tiraṇṭa tōḷpā rāṭṭi
yāyu mavaṉē yeṉṉum yāmum
vallē varuka varinta nāḷeṉa
nalliṟi melviral kūppi
illuṟi kaṭavuṭ kōkkutum paliyē.	(akam. 282)
itaṉuḷ tōḷpārāṭṭi yāyumavaṉē eṉṉum eṉ yāy teyvam parāyiṉāḷeṉpatupaṭak kūṟi, yām at teyvattiṟkup palikoṭuttu meṉṟavāṟu kāṇka:
pōkkuṭaṉ aṟintapiṉ tōḻiyoṭu keḻīik kaṟpiṉ ākkattu niṟṟaṟkaṇṇum - uṭaṉpōkku aṟinta piṉṉarc cevili tōḻiyoṭu matiyuṭampaṭṭu niṉṟu taliviyatu kaṟpu mikutiyē karuti uvanta uvakikkaṇṇum:
atu,
		emmaṉi muntuṟat tarumō
taṉmaṉi yuykkumō yātavaṉ kuṟippē	(akam. 195)
eṉṟāṟpōlak kaṟpiṉākkattuk karuttu nikaḻtal.
u-m:		muyaṅkukam vārāy tōḻi tayaṅkupu
kaṭalpeyarn taṉṉa kāṉalaṅ kalleṉap
peyalkaṭik koṇṭa peruntaṇ vāṭi
varumuli veppaṅ koḻunaṟ pōṟṟiya
ceṉṟaṉaḷ ammaniṉ tōḻi yavaṉō
ṭeṉṟiṉi varūum eṉṟaṉaḷ
valanturi tavirntaṉ ṟalarnta vūrē.
itu, cevili kaṟpiṉākkattu niṉṟami tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
piriviṉ eccattum - talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻit tāṉ piṉ cellātē eñcutalum uḷavātaliṉ āṇṭuk kūṟuvaṉavum;
u-m:		teṟuva tamma nummakaḷ viruppē

nāṭiṭi vilaṅkiya veṟpiṟ
kāṭiṟan taṉaḷnam kāta lōḷē.	(aiṅkuṟu. 313)
itu, piṉcellātu varuntiyirunta ceviliyik kaṇṭa naṟṟāy kūṟiyatu.

makaḷ neñcu valippiṉum - uṭaṉpōkkiṟku makaḷ neñcu tuṇiyiṉum:
taṉmēl aṉpu nīṅkiyatu uṇarntu cevili kūṟum.
u-m:		peyarttaṉeṉ muyaṅkayāṉ viyarttaṉe ṉeṉṟaṉaṉ

eṉa varum.

iṉṉavakiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyōṭu aṉṉavi piṟavum-ittaṉmittākiya kūṟapāṭṭiyuṭiya patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyōṭē avipōlvaṉa piṟavāy varuvaṉavum; cevili mēṉa - taṉ kūṟṟāyum piṟarkoṇṭu kūṟuṅ kūṟṟāyum kūṟum kūṟṟuc cevilikku uriyavām (e - ṟu).

		paṟipaṭap paṇila mārppa viṟikoḷpu

	(naṟṟiṇi, 55)
eṉṟalum pōlvaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka.	(24)
āyvuri
itu cevilikkuriya kūṟṟuvakiyiṉi uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉākiya niliyil ūrār alar kūṟiya kālattum, taliviyiṉ vēṭki aḷaviṟanta niliyilum, avaṉatu vaṉappu aḷavu mikkut tōṉṟiya kālattum. talivaṉoṭu taliviyi uṭaṉkaṇṭa kālattum, taliviyiṉ melivukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvippittum kaḻaṅku pārttum kaṇṭaṟinta avaḷatu melivu tīra vēlaṉik koṇṭu veṟiyāṭuvittal vēṇṭum eṉa iruvarum poruntiya pakkattuc ceytik kaṇṇum, aṅṅaṉam veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttat toṭaṅkiya niliyil tōḻi taṭuttu niṟuttiya niliyilum, kātal mikutiyāl talivaṉi niṉintu talivi kaṉavil araṟṟiya poḻutum ivipaṟṟit tōḻiyi viṉavutalum (iṉṉavāṟu nikaḻntateṉat tōḻi kūṟiyavaḻi ivviruvaratu oḻukalāṟṟiṉāl kuṭikkup paḻi viḷitalākātu eṉat) teyvatti vēṇṭutalum, talivi talivaṉoṭu uṭaṉpōyiṉāḷ eṉṟu aṟinta niliyil tōḻiyoṭu ārāyntu avviruvariyum maṉiyaṟattiṉkaṇ niṟuttaṟ kaṇṇum, talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṟ pirinta kālatatut talivi kaṟpu vaḻippaṭṭu maṉikkaṇ amitiyāka aṭaṅkiyirukkum avaḷatu neñcat tiṇmiyi eṇṇiya niliyilum,talivaṉatu kuṭippiṟappu talimakaḷatu kuṭippiṟap piṉoṭu okkum eṉa ārāytaṟ kaṇṇum ittaṉmiyavāya patiṉmūṉṟu vakik kūṟṟukkaḷuṭaṉ avi pēlvaṉa piṟavum cevilikkuriyaṉavām e-ṟu.
ivi cevilitāṉē kūṟum taṉ kūṟṟākavum taliviyum tōḻiyum cevilikūṟṟākak koṇṭeṭuttu moḻiyappaṭuvaṉa vākavum cevilikku uriyavām eṉpār cevilimēṉa eṉṟār.
26.	tāykkum variyār uṇarvuṭam paṭiṉē
iḷampūraṇam
itu naṟṟāykku uriyatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) ceviliyuṇarvōṭu uṭampaṭṭa vuḷḷattaḷāyiṉ, naṟṟāykkum mēṟcollappaṭṭavi yellām variyap paṭā veṉṟavāṟu.
uṇarvuṭam paṭutalāvatu---taliviyi yuṟṟu nōkkam nikaḻntavaḻi yaṉṟit tāṉum ceviliyippōla uṟṟu nōkkā ḷeṉṟu koḷka. ipporuḷmēṟ kiḷavi varuvaṉa uḷavēṉuñ ceviliyippōla voruppaṭṭa vuḷḷattaḷāyiṉ avaḷ kaṇṇum ikkiḷaviyellām nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟu. uṭampaṭāta vāṟu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, yāriṭattum makkaḷi vaḷarppār ceviliyarākalāṉum tamakkut tam illaṟanilikkuk kaṭava pakutiyāṉa aṟaṉum poruḷum iṉpamum vēṇṭutalāṉum kūṟṟoṭu vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟātu.	(26)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu, cevilikku uriyaṉa kūṟi naṟṟāykku uriya kūṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇarvu uṭampaṭiṉ - aṅṅaṉañ cevili uṇarn tāṅkē naṟṟāyum matiyuṭampaṭil; tāykkum variyār- naṟṟāykkum muṟkūṟiya patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyum piṟavumākak koṇṭu eṭuttu moḻital variyār (e - ṟu).
tāykkum eṉṟār, ivaṭku attuṇi payiṉṟuvārā eṉṟaṟku. atu naṟṟāy illaṟam nikaḻttuṅ karuttu vēṟu uṭimiyiṉ uṟṟunōkkāḷ; ceviliyē taliviyi uṟṟu nōkki oḻukuvaḷātaliṉ. ilakkaṇamuṇmiyiṉ ilakkiyam vantuḻik kāṇka1.	(25)
āyvuri
naṟṟāykkuriyatōr ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceviliyuṇarvuṭaṉ otta uṇarviṉaḷāyiṉ naṟṟāykkum mēṟcollappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷavikaḷum avipōl vaṉa piṟavum nīkkātu koḷḷat takkaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
uṇarvuṭampaṭutalāvatu taliviyiṉ oḻukalāṟṟic cevili. maṇarntavāṟē naṟṟāyum uṇarntu matiyuṭampaṭutal.
27.	kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ iruḷeṉa
miyaṟu ciṟappiṉ uyarntōr pāṅkiṉ
aiyak kiḷaviyiṉ aṟitalum urittē.
itu naṟṟāyum tuṇiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ eṉpatu---tali makaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē oru pakkam etirkālam nōkkik kūṟiṉār pōlat tōṉṟum; oru pakkam iṟantakālam tōṉṟum. avaṉ aṟiyāta aṟivurimi pūṇṭu mayaṅkutal, avaḷ ettuṇiyum mayakkamilaḷ eṉavum, avaṉ poruṭṭu mayaṅkiṉāḷ eṉavumpaṭak kūṟutal, talivaṉ aṟiyāta aṟiviṉiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉak kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya uyarntōrmāṭṭu uḷatākiya aiyakkiḷaviyāl puṇarppaṟitalum urittu, cevilikkum naṟṟāykkum eṉṟavāṟu.
iḵtu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉak kuṟṟamaṟṟa tavari viṉāya vaḻi avar ivvāṟu paṭṭateṉa meykūṟutalun takutiyaṉṟām : poy kūṟutalum takutiyaṉṟām. ātalāl aiyappaṭumāṟu kūṟiyavaḻi, 
ataṉāṉē yuṇarpa eṉṟavāṟu. kiḻavōṉaṟiyā vaṟiviṉaḷeṉṟavāṟukūṟiyavaḻikkiḻavōṉetirppaṭa...ïwªj kālattuḷ talivaṉ uḷaṉ eṉṟavāṟām.	(27)
nacciṉarkkiṉiyam
iḵtu aṅṅaṉaṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉar naṟṟāyk kuñ cevilikkum uriyatōr ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ ivaḷeṉa1 aiyak kiḷavi - naṅkulattiṟku otta talivaṉi mṟintuTṭātamṟiviṉiíṭiyaḷïvaḷeṉṟujmkṉattiIyamuṟṟumãṟarōṭuccāvuṅ»ḷaviyi;imayaṟuáṟappiṉcyarntōrgṅkiṉmṟitalumcrittē- FṟṟamaṟṟaáṟappiṉiyuṭiyamntaṇarKtaliyōriṭattēTṟimtuvumKṟimiyeṉṟumvarTṟamṟitalumcrittu(e- W).v‹wJ, mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivari yiṉṟē eṉa muṟ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ talivaṉ taṉ kulattiṉ uyarntami aṟinta viṭattu, iṅṅaṉam kūṭutal muṟiyaṉṟeṉṟu aiyuṟṟa ceviliyum naṟṟāyum, uyarntōrik kēṭṭu ituvuṅ kūṭumuṟimi eṉṟuṇarvar eṉpatām. ilakkaṇamuṉmiyiṉ ilakkiyamum akkālattu uḷaveṉṟuṇarka.2	(26)
āyvuri
itu, naṟṟāyum ceviliyum talimakaḷatu oḻukalāṟṟi aiyuṟṟut tuṇiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) talimakaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu kuṟṟam aṟutta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uṇarntōr pakkattu uḷatākiya aiyak kiḷaviyāl talivaṉōṭu talivikkuḷatākiya puṇarppiṉi yaṟintukoḷḷutal cevilikkum naṟṟāykkum uriyatu e- ṟu.
taliviyiṉ meliviṉik kaṇṭu varuntiya ceviliyum naṟṟāyum kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uyarntōrākiya aṟivarip paṇintu tam makaḷatu melivu etaṉālāyiṟṟu eṉa viṉavi niṟpar. anniliyil mukkāla nikaḻccikaḷiyum oruṅkuṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭiya apperiyōrkaḷ ivaḷ ittaṉmiyāṉ oruvaṉik kātalikkiṉṟāḷ eṉat taliviyōṭu talivaṉuk kuṇṭākiya toṭarpiṉi veḷippaṭac collutal mara paṉmiyāṉum, nikaḻntataṉi maṟittal tamatu vāymikku māṟātalāṉum num makaḷ talimakaḷ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu aiyuṟutaṟkuriya kavartta poruḷuṭiya toṭarāl maṟumoḻi kūṟuvar. etirkālattil taṉṉi maṇantu koḷḷum urimiyuṭiya kaṇavaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta pēraṟi viṉiyuṭiyāḷ num makaḷākiya ivaḷ eṉa veḷippaṭiyāka oru poruḷum, taṉ kātaṟ kiḻamiyuṭiyavaṉāka oḻukum talimakaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta aṟivurimi pūṇṭu avaṉ poruṭṭu mayaṅkukiṉṟāḷ ivaḷ eṉak kuṟippāka maṟṟoru poruḷum tarum niliyil aṟivar kūṟiya aiyakkiḷaviyi uyttuṇarntu talivaṉoruvaṉuṭaṉ tammakaḷukku uḷatākiya toṭarpiṉic ceviliyum naṟṟāyum aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkum uriyar eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉāl aṟivuṟuttappaṭum nuṇ poruḷākum.
28.	taṉṉuṟu vēṭki kiḻavaṉmuṟ kiḷattal
eṇṇuṅ kālik kiḻattik killip
piṟanīr mākkaḷiṉ aṟiya āyiṭip1
peynnīr pōlum uṇarviṟ ṟeṉpa.
iḷampūraṇam
itu talivikku uriyatōr iyalpuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) talivi taṉatu vēṭkiyik kiḻavaṉ muṉpu collutal niṉikkuṅ kālattuk kiḻattikku illi. aṅṅaṉam collāta viṭattum putukkalattiṉkaṭ peyta nīrpōlap puṟam pocintu kāṭṭum uṇarviṉiyu muṭittu avvēṭki eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, kuṟippiṉ uṇaraniṟkum eṉṟavāṟu. talivaṉ māṭṭuk kūṟṟiṉāṉum nikaḻappaṭumeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.1	(28)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu talivikku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) piṟanīr mākkaḷiṉ - vēṟuvēṟākat tammil tām kātalceytu oḻukum aṟivillātārip pōla; kiḻavaṉ aṟiyat taṉṉuṟu vēṭki muṟkiḷattal kiḻattikku illi - talivaṉ aṟiyumpaṭiyākat taṉakkuṟṟa vēṭkiyi avaṉ muṉṉark kūṟutal talivikku illi; āyiṭi - aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟṟillāta viṭattu; eṇṇuṅ kāliyum-avaḷ vēṭkiyi avaṉ ārāyuṅkāliyum; peynnīr pōlum uṇarviṟṟu eṉpa - avvēṭki putukkalattu peyta nīr puṟattup pocintu kāṭṭu māṟupōlum uṇarviṉi yuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar (e - ṟu).
kiḻattikkilli yeṉavē tōḻikkut talivi taṉatu vēṭkiyi etirniṉṟu kūṟutaluḷateṉpatu peṟṟām.2 talivikkuk kuṟippāṉaṉṟit talivaṉmuṉṉiṉṟu kūṟum vēṭkik kūṟṟiṉmi muṟkūṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷuṭ kāṇka. eṉavē, tōḻi; muṉṉark talivikku vēṭkikkūṟṟu nikaḻtal peṟṟām.
utāraṇam :
cēṇōṉ māṭṭiya naṟumpuki	(kuṟun. 150)
īyaṟ puṟṟat tīrmpuṟat tiṟutta	(akam)
eṉapaṉavum,
ivaḷē niṉcoṟkoṇṭa	(kuṟun. 81)
eṉṟāṟpōl varuvaṉavum muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām.
		kaṭumpuṉaṉ malinta kāvirip pēriyāṟṟu
neṭuñcuḻi nīttam maṇṇunaḷ pōla
naṭuṅkañar tīra muyaṅki nerunal
ākam aṭitan tōḷē............	(akam. 62,9-12)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa talivi vēṭkiyit talivaṉ kuṟippāṉ uṇarntaṉa.	(27)
āyvuri
itu, talivikku uriyatōr ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟuvēṟākak kātal ceytoḻukum aṟivillā tārip pōṉṟu palarkkum pulaṉākat taṉatu mikka vēṭkiyit talivaṉ muṉṉarc colluñcol, niṉikkuṅkālattut taliviyiṭattu nikaḻtal illi. avviṭattut taliviyatu vēṭki, putuk kalattup peyta nīr puṟampocintu kāṭṭumāṟu pōlum uṇarviṉiyuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
piṟanīr mākkaḷiṉ aṟiyat taṉ uṟu vēṭki kiḻavaṉ muṉ kiḷattal kiḻattikku illi eṉa iyiyum. piṟanīr mākkaḷ eṉṟatu, otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyamāy ekkālattum oṉṟupaṭṭu vāḻum aṉpiṉṟit tām kūṭiyirukkum kālattum tammi vēṟu vēṟākak karuti vēṭki vayappaṭṭu maṉam ceṉṟa vaḻiyoḻukum taṉmiyiṉarāya maṭavōri. mākkaḷiṉ eṉpuḻi iṉ uvamavurupu. aṟiya palarkkum pulaṉāka. peynnīr eṉpataṟkēṟpap putuvatāka nīr peytaṟkuriya putukkalam eṉpatu varuvitturikkap paṭṭatu.
29.	kāmak kūṭṭan taṉimiyiṟ politaliṉ1
tāmē tūtuva rākalum urittē.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu, kaḷaviṟpuṇarccik kuriyatoru vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ ) mēṟ coṉṉavāṟṟāl pāṅkaṉuntōḻiyu nimitta-mākak kūṭutaleyaṉṟit tāmē tūtuvarākiya kūṭṭaṅkaḷ nikaḻap peṟum. atu ciṟappuṭittātalāl eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉpaṉa niyamamilli; yārmāṭṭum eṉṟavāṟām. taṉimiyiṟ politaliṉ eṉṟamiyāṉ itu mikavum naṉṟu.	(29)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
iḵtu eytātatu eytuḷittatu, pāṅkaṉun tōḻiyum nimittamāka vaṉṟit tāmē tūtākum iṭamum uṇṭeṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) kāmakkūṭṭan taṉimiyiṟ politaliṉ - iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum iṭantalippāṭuṅ kūṭṭuvāriyiṉṟit taṉimiyāṟ polivupeṟutaliṉ; tāmē tūtuvarāki oruvar oruvarik kūṭutalum āṇṭurittu (e - ṟu).
atu meyppāṭṭiṉuṭ pukumukam puritaṉ (261) mutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭāṉum aṟika. itaṉ payaṉ ik iṭṭattiṉ piṉṉar varitalum uṇṭeṉpatāl.1	(28)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷaviṟpuṇarccikku uriyatōr tiṟam uṇarttukiṉṟatu
(i-ḷ.) iyaṟkip puṇarcciyākiya kūṭṭam kūṭṭivippār piṟariṉṟit talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvarum piṟaraṟiyātavāṟu nikaḻum taṉimiyuṭiyatāyp polivuṟuvatātaliṉ, talivaṉ talivi iruvarum tattamatu uḷakkaruttip pulappaṭuttum tūtu-varākat taṉimiyil muyaṉṟu kūṭutalum uṇṭu e---ṟu.
tām eṉṟatu, talivaṉ talivi iruvariyum. tāmē eṉpuḻi ēkāram tōḻi mutaliya vāyilkaḷip piritataliṉ pirinili. talivaṉ taliviyiruvarum cērntu tāmē tūtavarāy niṉṟu kūṭum nili, meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ pukamukam purital mutaliya meyppāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭāṉum. naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. eṉavē talivaṉ pāṅkaṉatu utavipeṟṟuk kūṭutalum tōḻiyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟuk kūṭutalumākiya ivi yāvariṭattum tavaṟātu nikaḻavēṇṭum eṉṉum variyaṟiyilliyeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
30.	avaṉvaram piṟatta laṟantaṉak kiṉmiyiṉ1
kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākun
tāṉcelaṟ kuriyavaḻi yāka lāṉa
iḷampūraṇam
itu collappaṭṭa kūṭṭattiṟkuk kuṟiyiṭam kūṟuvā ṉuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta piṉpu, talivaṉelliyi maṟattal talivikku aṟamāka liṉmiyāṉē kuṟiyiṭam kūṟutal talimakaḷtām ; atu tāṉ cēṟaṟ kuriya iṭamātalāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ittuṇikkūṟiṉ mikiyaṉṟu eṉṟavāṟām.2 (30)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu muṉṉark kaḷaṉum poḻutum eṉṟavaṟṟuṭ kaḷaṅkūṟu taṟkum uriyāḷ taliviyeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṉ varampu iṟattal - talivaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉ elliyik kaṭattal; taṉakku aṟamiṉmiyiṉ-talivikku uritteṉak kūṟiya tarumanūliṉmiyiṉ; kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatu ākum-talimakaṉi iṉṉaviṭattu varuka veṉṟu ōriṭattit tāṉ karutik kūṟuṅkūṟṟu avaṉ kuṟippuvaḻi oḻukun taliviyatām; tāṉ celaṟku uriyavaḻi ākalāṉ-tāṉ ceṉṟu kūṭutaṟkuriya iṭantāṉē uṇarvaḷ ātalāṉ (e-ṟu).
cuṭṭukkiḷavi eṉṟataṉ karuttut talivaṉ iruvakik kuṟiyum vēṇṭiyavaḻi avaṉi maṟātu tāṉaṟinta viṭattiṉik kūṟṟāṉaṉṟik kuṟippāṉātal ciṟippuṟattāṉātal tōḻiyāṉātal uṇarttumeṉpatām. talivaṉ kaḷañcuṭṭu māyiṉ yāṇṭāṉum eppoḻutāṉum ak kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻntu piṟarkkum pulaṉāyk kuṭippiṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut takātām. viriyiṇar vēṅki eṉṉum (38) akappāṭṭut talivi kaḷañcuṭṭiyatu : maṟanticiṉ yāṉē eṉṟaliṉ itu kuṟippāṉa uṇarttiṟṟu. piṟavum vantuḻik kāṇka.	(29)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavoḻukkattiṟ kuṟiyiṭamituveṉak kuṟikkum poṟuppu talimakaṭkuriyatu eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ talivaṉatu colliṉ varampiṉik kaṭattal talimakaṭku aṟamaṉṟākalāṉum tāṉ cellutaṟkuriya vaḻi avviṭamātalāṉum tāṅkaḷ iruvarum mīṇṭum kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuriya iṭattik kuṟippiṭum poṟuppu talimakaḷic cārntatākum e-ṟu.
avaṉ eṉṟatu, talivaṉi. varampu eṉṟatu avaṉ paṇitta collākiya elli. iṟattal - kaṭattal. aṟam-kaṟpaṟamākiya neṟimuṟi. taṉakku aṟamaṉmiyiṉ eṉa iṉṉum, tāṉ celaṟkuriya vaḻi ākalāṉ eṉa āṉum ētup poruḷil vantaṉa.1
taliviyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟut tōḻiyāṟ kuṟiyiṭam cuṭṭappaṭṭut talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭum iṭam eṉṟatu, pāṅkiyiṉ utavipeṟṟut talimakaḷik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvum pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ pakaṟkuṟi iravuk kuṟiyākiya iṭaṅkaḷi.
31.	tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟ kāyatoru ciṟappuviti uṇarttu tal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēl, kāmakkūṭṭan taṉimiyiṟ politaliṟṟāmē tūtuva rākalum urit teṉak kūṟippōntār; avvāṟaṉṟi mēṟ collappaṭṭa iyaṟkip puṇarcciyāvatu tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyu miṭattu ōriṭattu uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu. aṉpoṭu puṇarnta vintiṇi (iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal-1) eṉṟataṉāl yāṇṭum uḷḷap puṇarcciyāṉ vēṭkimī tūrnta vaḻiyē tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyap peṟuvatu eṉṟu koḷka.1 allākkāṟ peruntiṇip pāṟpaṭum.2	(31)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu tōḻikkuṅ kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi uritteṉṟu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi talivi kuṟippāl tōḻi kūṟutalaṉṟit tāṉēyuṅ kūṟappeṟum orōvaḻi eṉṟavāṟu. tōḻi kuṟitta iṭamun talivi tāṉ cēṟaṟkuriya iṭamāmeṉpatu karuttu.3
		vaṇṭupaṭa
virinta cerunti veṇmaṇaṉ muṭukkarp
pūvēy puṉṉiyan taṇpoḻil
vāvē teyya maṇantaṉi celaṟkē	(akam. 240)
eṉat tōḻi kaḷañcuṭṭiyavāṟum kāṇka.	(30)
āyvuri
itu, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟkuriyatōr ciṟappu viti kūṟu- kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tōḻiyāṟ kuṟiyiṭam aṟivikkappaṭṭup poruntu miṭamum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
32.	munnā ḷallatu tuṇiyiṉṟu kaḻiyātu
annā ḷakattum atuvari viṉṟē.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻumiṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēl, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ vikaṟpaṅkūṟiṉā rākaliṉ, īṇṭut tuṇi yeṉṟatu pāṅkaṉ āyiṟṟu.1 mūṉṟu nāḷallatu tuṇiyiṉaṟik kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci cellātu;2 an nāḷakattum tuṇiyi nīkkavum paṭātu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarvataṉ muṉ pāṅkaṟku uṇarttavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭatu.	(32)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
ituvum atikārattāl talivikkeytiyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) munnāḷ allatu tuṇiyiṉṟu kaḻiyātu pūp peytiya mūṉṟunāḷum allatu kūṭṭamiṉṟi1 ikkaḷa voḻukkam kaḻiyātu; annāḷakattum atu varivu iṉṟē am mūṉṟunāḷil akappaṭṭa nāḷākiya orunāḷiṉum iraṇṭu nāḷiṉun tuṇi yiṉṟik kaḻital nīkkappaṭātu (e-ṟu).
atu eṉṟatu tuṇiyiṉṟu kaḻitali2. pūppiṉāl tuṇi yiṉṟik kaḻital poruntiṟṟāyiṉum, pūppiṉṟi orunāḷum iraṇṭunāḷun tuṇiyiṉṟik kaḻital vaḻuvāmeṉak karutiṉ atuvum puṟattārkkup pulaṉām eṉṟu añcutalāṟ kaḻitaliṉ vaḻuvākāveṉṟaṟku variviṉṟu eṉṟār, iṉṉōraṉṉa kāraṇan talivaṟkiṉmiyiṉ avaṉāl tuṇiyiṉṟik kaḻital iṉṟāyiṟṟu.
u-m:		kukkū veṉṟatu kōḻi ataṉetir
tuṭkeṉ ṟaṟṟeṉ ṟūu neñcam
tōṭōy kātalarp pirikkum
vāḷpōl vikaṟi vantaṉṟā leṉavē	(kuṟun. 157)
itu munnāḷip pirivākiya pūppiṭip pirivu vantuḻit talivi kūṟiyatu.
iṉi allakuṟippaṭṭuḻi orunāḷum iraṇṭunāḷum iṭiyīṭāmeṉṟuṇarka. pūppunikaḻāta kālattuk kaḷavoḻukkam pūppu nikaḻkālam variyappaṭṭateṉṟu urippārum uḷar; iv viti antaṇarkkuk kūṟiyataṉṟu; aracar vaṇikarātiyavarkkuc ciṟupāṉmiyākavum, ēṉi vēḷāḷar, āyar, vēṭṭuvar mutali yōrkkup perumpāṉmiyākavuṅ kūṟiya vitiyeṉṟuṇarka.1 eṉṉi? pūppu nikaḻuṅkālattu variyātu kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻt tiṉārkku.
		antarat teḻutiya veḻuttiṉ māṉa
vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa voḻukalum	(tol. kaṟpiyal, 5)
eṉpataṉāṟ pirāyaccittam vitippārātaliṉ.
itaṉāṉē antaṇar makaḷirkkum pūppeytiyakkāl aṟattoṭu niṉṟum varital peṟutum.	(31)
āyvuri
itu, tōḻiyiṉ tuṇipeṟṟālaṉṟik kaḷavoḻukkam toṭarntu nikaḻtal iyalātu eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ eṉṉum kāla ellikku uṭpaṭṭallatu tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟit toṭarntu nikaḻātu, mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷākiya kāla ellikkuḷḷum tōḻiyiṉ tuṇi kiṭikkumāyiṉ variviṉṟi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭum e-ṟu.
iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum mūṉṟu nāḷ elliyaḷavum talivaṉ tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟit talimakaḷik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutal kūṭum eṉpatum, mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukku mēlāyiṉ tōḻiyiṉ iciviṉṟit talimakaṉ talimakaḷi etirppaṭutal aritu eṉpatum, mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷākiya avvellikkuḷḷēyē tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiyiṉip peṟutalum uṇṭeṉpatum innūṟpāvāṟ pulaṉām.
iṅkut tuṇi eṉṟatu tōḻiyi. itu, tuṇic cuṭṭuk kiḷavi eṉa aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvāl iṉitu pulaṉām.
33.	paṉṉūṟu vakiyiṉun taṉvayiṉ varūum
naṉṉaya maruṅkiṉ nāṭṭam vēṇṭalil
tuṇiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākun1
tuṇiyōr karuma māta lāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, talivikku uriyatō riyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) paṉṉūṟu vakiyiṉum eṉpatu - palavaki yāṉum eṉṟavāṟu. nūṟu, pattu, āyiram eṉpaṉa palporuṭ peyar.

taṉvayiṉvarūu...khjyhd eṉpatu---taṉṉiṭattu varum nallanayappāṭṭu¥pakkattidārāytaštaliva‹māṭLvēṇṭumātalāl,tuṇiyi¢cuṭṭikkūṟaluṟu«coštaymakaḷatākum;tā‹kūṟu«karuma«tuṇiyōrā‰ceyyappaṭu«karumamātalā‹eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, talimakaṉ kaḷavukkālattup pāṅkaṟku uṟṟaturitta piṉpu pāṅkaṉic cuṭṭi yātu ceyvāmeṉak kūṟappeṟum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.2	(33)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu, talivikku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) taṉvayiṉ varūum naṉṉaya maruṅkiṉ - talivi yiṭattē tōḻikkuñ cevilikkum varukiṉṟa aṉpu mikutik kaṇṇē; palnūṟu vakiyiṉum nāṭṭam vēṇṭalil - pala nūṟākiya pakutiyāṉum ākkamuṅ kēṭum ārāytali avar virumputalālē; tuṇiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatākum-ivaḷ oru tuṇiyuṭiyaḷeṉa avar cuṭṭutaliṭattuk kiḷakkuṅkiḷavi taliviyatām; tuṇiyōr karumam ākalāṉa - ak kiḷavi at tōḻiyāṉuñ ceviliyāṉum muṭiyuṅkāriyam ākalāṉ (e-ṟu).
eṉṟatu, tōḻi palavēṟu kavarporuṇāṭṭam (114) uṟṟavaḻiyuñ cevili kaḷavu alarātal mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ (115) nāṭṭamuṟṟa vaḻiyun talivi aṟattoṭu niṟkumeṉṟu aṟattoṭu nilikku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.1 tōḻikkut talivi aṟattoṭu niṟpa, avaḷ cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpa. avaḷ naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟkumeṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉāṉē pāṅkaṟku uṟṟaturitta piṉṉart talivaṉ uriyāmiyum peṟṟām2. puṉiyiḻi nōkkiyum (kali. 76) eṉṉum marutak kaliyuḷ, viṉavutiyāyiṉ eṉa nāṭṭam nikaḻntavāṟum, ataṉ curitakattuk kūṭṭamuṇmi kūṟutaliṉ tuṇiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatāyavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
koṭiyavuṅ kōṭṭavum (kali. 54.) eṉpataṉ curitakattuc cevilikku uriyāyeṉak kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka.	(32)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavoḻukkattukkuriya tuṇiyiṉic cuṭṭutal taliviyiṉ kaṭami eṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palnūṟu vakiyālun talimakaḷ pāl uḷavākum naṉmip pakutiyākiyavirumpattakka cūḻnilikaḷi ūṉṟi nōkkum uṇarvu talivaṉukku vēṇṭumātalāṉum, tāṅkaḷ iruvarum mēṟ koṇṭa aṉpiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa kaḷavoḻukkam oṉṟiya aṉpuṭiya tuṇiyākiya tōḻiyāl puṟattāraṟiyātu toṭarntu nikaḻtal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, taṉakkut tuṇiyāvāḷ ivaḷ eṉat taṉ āruyirt tōḻiyit talivaṉukkuccuṭṭik kūṟuñcol talivikku uriyatākum e-ṟu.
talimakaḷukku naṉmikaḷ uḷavātaṟkuriya tiṟaṅkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiyum eñcāmal tokuttut kūṟum muṟiyiṟ paṉṉūṟu vakiyiṉum eṉṟār. nāṭṭam - ūṉṟinōkkum uṇarvu. tuṇiyōr karumam - uḷḷam oṉṟiya naṇpuṭiya tuṇivarāl niṟivēṟṟutaṟkuriya ceyal. nāṭṭam vēṇṭaliṉ eṉpuḻi iṉṉum, tuṇiyōrk karumam ākalāṉ eṉpuḻi āṉum ētup poruḷil vantaṉa.
34.	āyperuñ ciṟappiṉ arumaṟi kiḷattaliṉ
tāyeṉap paṭuvāḷ cevili yākum.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, cevilikku uriyatoru ciṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) nalla periya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya aṟitaṟkariya maṟipporuḷ yāvaṟṟiyuṅ kūṟum kaṭappāṭuṭiyaḷātaliṉ tāy eṉappaṭuvāḷ ceviliyākum eṉṟavāṟu.

(i - ḷ.) āyperuñ ciṟappiṉ - tāyttāykkoṇṭu uyir oṉṟāyvarukiṉṟāḷeṉṟu1 ārāyntu tuṇiyappaṭṭa peruñ ciṟappuk kāraṇamāka; arumaṟi kiḷattaliṉ - kūṟutaṟ kariya maṟiporuḷellāṅ kuṟippāṉaṉṟik kūṟṟāṟ kūṟat takkāḷātaliṉ; tāyeṉappaṭuvāḷ cevili ākum - tāyeṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭuvāḷ ceviliyēyām (e- W).vdnt īṉṟa tāyiṉum kaḷaviṉkaṭ ciṟantāḷ ivaḷeṉṟār2. kaṟpiṟku iruvarum oppārāyiṟṟu. cevili ciṟantami cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ palaruṅ kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.	(33)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavoḻukkattil tāy eṉak kuṟikkappaṭupavaḷ ceviliyē eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ārāytaṟkuriya peruñciṟappiṉiyuṭiya arumi vāyntatāy ulakattāraṟiyātu maṟivil nikaḻum aṉpurimi oḻukalāṟṟiṉik kūṟutaṟkurimi yuṭiyavaḷātaliṉ (talivi tōḻi eṉṉum iruvarālum) tāy eṉac ciṟappākak kūṟappaṭupavaḷ cevilittāy āvāḷ e-ṟu.
aṟivāl nirampiya cāṉṟōrkaḷāl ārāyntu tuṇitaṟ kuriya peruñ ciṟappuṭiyatāy otta aṉpiṉarākiya oruvaṉ orutti eṉṉum iruvariṭiyē maṟiyil nikamum kēṇmi eṉpār, āy peruñciṟappiṉ arumaṟi eṉṟār maṟinta oḻukkamākiya itaṉi uyttuṇarntu tōḻiyi viṉavik kēṭṭaṟkum avvarumaṟiyi. naṟṟāykkuk kūṟutaṟkum uriya paḻaṅkēṇmiyuṭiyāḷ cevili eṉpār, arumaṟikiḷattaliṉ tāyeṉappaṭuvōḷ ceviliyākum eṉṟār. kiḷattal eṉṟatu tōḻiyāṟ cevilikkuk kiḷakkap paṭutaliyum naṟṟāykkuc cevili kiḷattaliyum potuppaṭac cuṭṭi niṉṟatu.
35.	tōḻi tāṉē cevili makaḷē.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, tōḻikku uriyatoru ciṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavukkālattum iṉṟiyamiyāḷākat taliviyāl vēṇṭappaṭṭāḷ cevilimakaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, payiṉṟā rellārun tōḻiyarākār. arumaṟi kiḷakkap paṭutalāṉ uṭaṉ muliyuṇṭu vaḷarnta cevili makaḷē tōḻi eṉappaṭuvāḷ eṉṟavāṟu. arumaṟi kiḷattal eṉpataṉi yīṇṭu varuvikka1
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu tōḻiyatu ciṟappuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tōḻi tāṉē - tōḻiyar palaruḷḷum orutti yeṉap pirikkappaṭuvāḷ; cevili makaḷē-muṟkūṟiya cevili yuṭiya makaḷ (e-ṟu)
itaṟkum arumaṟi kiḷattal atikārattāṟ koḷka.2 tāyttāyk koṇṭu varukiṉṟamiyiṉ uḻuvalaṉpu pōlva tōr aṉpu uṭiyar iruvarumeṉṟu koḷka. itaṉāṉē kaḷaviṟkut tōḻiyē ciṟantāḷāyiṟṟu; atu cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ kāṇka.	(34)
āyvuri
itu, tōḻiyāvāḷ ivaḷ eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) talimakaḷatu uṇarvuṭaṉ oṉṟiya vuṇarviṉaḷākiya tōḻiyāvāḷ ceviliyiṉ makaḷ e-ṟu.
tōḻitāṉē eṉpuḻi ēkāram oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi eṉa akattiṇiyiyalilum tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ tōḻi eṉa ivviyalilum ciṟappitturikkappeṟṟa tōḻiyit taṉittu vāṅkik kūṟutaliṉ piriniliyēkāram.
36.	cūḻtalum ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṟ polimē.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, tōḻikku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟcollappaṭṭa tōḻi tāṉ cūḻtaṟkum talivi cūḻccikku ucāttuṇiyākiyum varum nilimiyāṟ polivu peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.1
eṉavē, cevilimakaḷ eṉṉun tuṇiyāṟ polivu peṟāḷ; eṉṟum tōḻiyāvāḷ cevilimakaḷātalēyaṉṟic cūḻavum ucāt tuṇiyākavum vallaḷ ātalvēṇṭum eṉṟa vāṟu.
ceyyuḷ mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.	(36)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
ituvun tōḻi ciṟappiṉiyē kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṉ - talimakaṉun tali makaḷum ucāvutaṟkut tuṇimicāṉṟa nilimiyiṉālē; cūḻtalum polimē-puṇarcci yuṇmiyi ēḻuvakiyāṉuñ cūḻtaṟ kaṇṇum polivupeṟum (e-ṟu).
eṉavē, immūṉṟu nilikkun tōḻi uriyaḷ eṉṟār.2 utāraṇam muṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuṭ kāṇka.	(35)
āyvuri
itu, tōḻikku iṉṟiyamiyātatōr iyalpuṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkuṟitta tōḻiyāvāḷ ceviliyiṉ makaḷ eṉṟa aḷavilaṉṟit talimakaḷik kuṟittut taṉ maṉattuḷḷē ārāytaṟkum, talimakaḷukkuc cūḻccittuṇiyāka iruntu aṟivuri vaḻaṅkutaṟkum uriya tōḻami nilimiyiṟ polivu peṟṟu viḷaṅkuvāḷ e-ṟu.
tōḻiyiṉ ciṟappilakkaṇam kūṟum ittoṭarum avaḷ iṉṉa toṭarpuṭiyāḷ eṉac cuṭṭum mēli nūṟpāvum tol kāppiyaṉārāl orē cūttiramāka iyaṟṟap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu,
		tōḻi tāṉē cevili makaḷē
cūḻtalum ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṟ polimē
eṉa ōretukiyil amintu oruporuḷ kuṟittu varutalāl iṉitu pulaṉākum. tōḻiyāvāḷ ivaḷ eṉavum avaḷatu ciṟappiyalpu ituveṉavum pakutturikkum nōkkil iru cūttiraṅkaḷākap pirittu urivariyap peṟṟateṉak koḷḷutal ēṟpuṭiyatākum. ucā --- cūḻcci; iṉic ceyya vēṇṭuvatu yātu eṉa eṇṇi ārāyum ārāycci. tuṇi-avvārāyccikkut tuṇiyāyiruntu aṟivuri pakartal. ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟākiya poliyum eṉpatu, īṟṟumiciyukaram meyyoṭu keṭṭup polim eṉa niṉṟatu. ceyyumeṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷavikkuīṟṟumiciyukarammeyyoṭum keṭum (tol.col.238) eṉavē ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟuc collukku īṟṟumiciyukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭum; meyyoḻintuṅkeṭum eṉa varum cēṉāvariyar uriviḷakkam iṅku niṉikkat takuvatākum,
37.	kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal muṉṉuṟa uṇartal
iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉvara vuṇartaleṉa
matiyuṭam paṭutal orumū vakittē.
iḷampūraṇam
itu talivaṉ puṇarcci yuṇmi tōḻi aṟiyun tiṟaṉ pākupaṭumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) talivaṉ kuṟiyaṟ vuṇartalum avaṉ kuṟi yuṟāvaḻit talivi kuṟippukkaṇṭu uṇartalum tāṉum taliviyuṅ kūṭiyiruntuḻit talivaṉ vantami kaṇṭu uṇartalum eṉa mūvakittut tōḻi aṟivuṭampaṭutaṟkaṇṇa eṉṟavāṟu.
matiyuṭampaṭutal eṉiṉum puṇarcciyuṇartal eṉiṉum okkum immūṉṟiṉum oṉṟu kaṇṭuḻi avaravar kuṟippiṉāṟ yuṇarcciyuṇarum eṉṟavāṟu. kuṟiyuṇartal muṉvittār naṉku pulappaṭutaliṉ. muṉṉuṟavuṇartal ataṉpiṉ vittār, talivi vēṟu pāṭukaṇṭu paṇṭiyiṟpōlāḷ eṉṉum nikaḻcciyāṉ muṟṟattuṇi viṉmiyiṉ, iruvaru muḷvaḻi yavaṉ varavuṇartal ataṉ piṉ vittār. āṇṭup putuvōṉpōlat talivaṉ varutalāṉum talivi karanta uḷḷattaḷāy niṟkumātalāṉum attuṇip pulap pāṭiṉmiyiṉ. akkaruttiṉāṉē mēṟ collappaṭṭa tōḻikūṟṟu mūvakiyākap poruḷ uritta teṉṟu koḷka.1	(37)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
iḵtu, attōḻi cūḻcci ittuṇip pakutittu eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal - talivaṉ tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiyuṟṟavaḻi uṇartal ; muṉ uṟa uṇartal - muṉṉam mika uṇartal ; iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartal - taliviyun tōḻiyum oruṅkiruntavaḻit talivaṉ varutalāl talivaṉ kuṟippun talivi kuṟippuṅ kaṇṭuṇartal ; eṉa matiyuṭam paṭuttal oru mūvakittē - eṉṟu iruvar karuttiṉiyun taṉ karuttiṉōṭu oṉṟupaṭuttuṇartal orumūṉṟu kūṟṟiṉiyuṭittu (e-ṟu).
eṇṇutal eṇṇeṉṟāṟpōla muṉṉutal muṉ ṉeṉa niṉṟatu. uyirkalantoṉṟaliṟ kuṟippiṉṟiyum pāka muṇarvāḷ kuṟippup peṟṟuḻi mikavuṇarum eṉṟu koḷka. itu mūvar mati yiṉiyum oṉṟupaṭuttutaliṉ matiyuṭampaṭuttaleṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu.1 im mūṉṟuṅ kūṭiya piṉṉarallatu matiyuṭampaṭutta liṉṟeṉṟaṟku mūvakitteṉṟu orumiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉuṟa veṉṟataṉi muntuṟa veṉṟālōveṉiṉ2 kuṟiyuṟutalāṉ uṇartal avaṉ varutalāṉ uṇartaleṉṟu iraṇṭaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟutaliṉ. itaṟkuṅ kuṟippumikutalāṉ uṇartaleṉak kāraṇaṅ koṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉṟuṇarka.3 nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum (tol. po. 114) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi muṉṉuṟavi iṭivittār, av viraṇṭiṉāṉ uṇaruṅkālum ik kuṟippāṉ uṇaravēṇṭu meṉṟaṟku. immūṉṟum muṟkūṟiṟrēṉum orōvoṉṟāṟ kūṭṭamuṇaril taliviyi naṉku matittilaḷāvaḷeṉṟaṟku im mūṉṟum vēṇṭumeṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.4
u-m:		kōṉē relvaḷi teḷirppa niṉpōl
yāṉu māṭik kāṇkō tōḻi	(pakkam. 99)
itu kūṭṭamuṇarātāḷpōla nāṇiṟku māṟākāmaṟ kūṟaliṉ muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal.
		niṉṉiṉ viṭāa niḻaṟpōṟ ṟiritaruvāy
eṉṉī peṟāta titeṉ.	(kali. 61)
itu kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal.
ēṉal kāva livaḷu mallaḷ
eṉpatu avaṉ varavuṇartal.	(36)
āyvuri
itu, pāṅkimatiyuṭampāṭu muttiṟappaṭṭu nikaḻum eṉkiṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ taṉpāl vantu irantu kuṟiyuṟṟu niṟṟalāl avaṉatu uḷḷak karuttiṉiyuṇartalum, taliviyiṉ uḷḷak kuṟippiṉik kaṇṭuṇartalum, taliviyum tāṉum oruṅ kirunta niliyil vara anniliyil avviruvaratu uḷḷak karut tiṉiyuṇartalum eṉat tōḻi, taṉ karuttuṭaṉ avviruvaratu karuttiṉiyum vittu oṉṟupaṭuttu uṇarum uṇarcci mūvakippaṭum eṉpar āciriyar e- ṟu.
pāṅkimatiyuṭampāṭāvatu talivaṉ talivi yeṉṉum kātalar iruvaratu aṟiviṉiyum tōḻi taṉṉaṟiviṉāl oruṅku vittu ārāyntu avviruvariṭiyē neruṅkiya toṭarpuṇmi tuṇital. 		muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal
iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉvara vuṇartaleṉ
ṟammūṉ ṟeṉpa tōḻik kuṇarcci (7)
eṉavarum nūṟpāvil iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalāciriyar eṭut tāṇṭuḷḷami iṅku oppunōkkiyuṇarattakuvatākum.
iṅṅaṉam pāṅki taṉ aṟiviṉuṭaṉ talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvar aṟiviṉiyum oṉṟupaṭuttu uṇartaliṉ matiyuṭam paṭuttal eṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. mati - aṟivu. uṭampaṭuttal - oṉṟu paṭutti uṇartal.
38.	aṉṉa vakiyāṉ uṇarntapiṉ allatu
piṉṉili muyaṟci peṟāḷeṉa1 moḻipa.
iḷampūraṇam
ituvum tōḻikku uriyatoru tiṟaṉ uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟcollappaṭṭa kūṟupāṭṭāṉa iruvar māṭṭum aṉpuṭimi uṇarntapiṉ allatu vaḻipāṭṭu nilimiyāṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muyalappeṟāḷ tōḻi eṉṟavāṟu.
aḵtēl, uḷḷappuṇarcciyāṉiṉṟu meyyuṟātu kūṭṭattiṟku muyalvār uḷar. āyiṉ, aḵteṟṟāṟpeṟum eṉiṉ āṇṭum iruvar māṭṭuḷatākiya aṉpuṭimiyāṉ maṉanikaḻcciyuḷavāka annikaḻcci kaṇṭuḻiyum muyalappeṟumeṉṟu koḷka. ataṉāṉēyaṉṟē muṉṉuṟavuṇartal eṉṉum cūttirattiṉum puṇarcci yuṭampaṭutal eṉṉātu matiyaṭampaṭuta loru mūvakiya eṉap potuppaṭa ōtuvārāyiṟ ṟeṉka avvaṉpiṉāṉ varu nikaḻcci yuḷḷavaḻiyum ivviṭamūṉṟiṉum kālamuṇmi aṟiyalākum.2	(38)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu matiyuṭampaṭṭa piṉṉallatu talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻt tap peṟāṉeṉkiṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉavakiyāṉ uṇarntapiṉ allatu-am mūvakiyāṉun tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭutta piṉṉallatu; piṉṉili ivaṉ oru kuṟiyuṭiyayeṉṟu tōḻi uytturi niṟkumiṭattu; muyaṟcipeṟāṉ eṉa moḻipa - kūṟṟāṉ akkuṟi muṭittal vēṇṭumeṉṟu muṭukkutal peṟāṉeṉṟu kūṟupa āciriyar (e-ṟu).
tōḻi taṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭavāṟu kaṇṭu matiyuṭampaṭṭa vāṟuṇarntu kūṟṟāṉ uṇarttum.1
atu nerunalu muṉṉāḷ eṉpataṉuḷ ārañarvaruttaṅ kaḷiyāyō eṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.	(37)
āyvuri
ituvum tōḻikkuriyatōr tiṟam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ ) mēṟkuṟitta mūvakiyālum talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvarpālum otta aṉpuṭimiyuṇarnta piṉṉaṉṟit taṉṉi irantu piṉṉiṟkum talivaṉatu muyaṟcikkut tōḻi iṭantaramāṭṭāḷ e-ṟu.
akaṉintiṇiyoḻukalāṟṟil iṭam peṟum tōḻiyiṉ ceṟivum niṟiyum cemmiyum aṟivum arumiyumākiya peṇmip paṇpukaḷ innūṟpāvāl pulappaṭuttappeṟṟami uṇartaṟ kuriyatākum. ittolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ karutti aṭiyoṟṟiyamintatu,
		muṉṉuṟa vuṇariṉum avaṉkuṟi yuṟṟa
piṉṉa rallatu kiḷavi tōṉṟātu	(9)
eṉa varum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalākum. matiyuṭampaṭuppak kūṭṭa muṇmiyuṇarnta tōḻi talivaṉ irantu piṉṉiṉṟa piṉṉaṉṟi avaṉ kuṟiyiṉi muṭippēṉ eṉak kūṟa māṭṭāḷ eṉpatām.
iṉi, piṉṉili muyaṟci peṟāṉ eṉap pāṭaṅ koṇṭu kuṟiyuṟavuṇartal mutalāka mūvakiyuṇarvāl talimakaḷoṭu taṉakkuḷḷa aṉpurimit toṭarpiṉit tōḻi uṇarntu vēṇṭit tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṟkum muyaṟciyi mēṟ koḷḷappeṟāṉ eṉap poruḷ varintu talivaṉatu maṟi piṟaraṟiyā niṟiyuṭimiyip pulappaṭuttiyatākak koḷḷutalum poruntum.
39.	muyaṟcik kālat tataṟpaṭa nāṭip
puṇartta lāṟṟalum avaḷvayi ṉāṉa.1
iḷampūraṇam
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ ) tōḻi vaḻimoḻintu muyaluṅkālattu avaṉ niṉiviṉkaṭpaṭuntiṟaṉ ārāyntu puṇarttalic ceyyum avaḷiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
aḵtāvatu, iṉṉuḻic cel eṉavum iṉṉuḻi vā eṉavum, taliviyi āyattuṇiṉṟum pirittut taṉi niṟuttip paṭṭāṅku2 kūṟiyum piṟavāṟṟāṉum ārāyntu kūṭṭutal.
ivvintu cūttirattāṉun tōḻikkuriya marapu uṇarttiya vāṟu kāṇka.	(39)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu talivaṉ muyaṟci kūṟiya muṟiyē tōḻi muyaṟci piṟakkumiṭaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muyaṟcikkālattu - talivaṉ aṅṅaṉaṅkūṭutaṟkumuyaṟcinikaḻttuṅkālattē;nāṭiataṟpaṭappuṇarttalum -talivikūṭutaṟkumuyaluṅkaruttiṉiārāyntuakkūṭṭattiṭattēuḷḷampaṭumpaṭikūṭṭutalum;avaḷvayiṉāṉāṟṟal -tōḻiyiṭattuuṇṭāṉakaṭippiṭi(e-ṟu.).3
āṟṟal oṉṟaṉi muṭivupōkkal.1 ummi, eccavummi.2 matiyuṭampaṭuttalē yaṉṟik kūṭṭavum peṟumeṉka.	(38)
āyvuri
ituvum tōḻikkuriyatōr tiṟam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ ) talivaṉ tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṉṟu taliviyi aṭiya muyaluṅkālattu ammuyaṟciyiṉ vaḻiniṉṟu (avviruvaratu uḷḷak karuttiṉiyum) ārāynta avviruvarum tammuṭkūṭi aṉpiṉāl aḷavaḷāvum vaṇṇam kūṭṭuvittalum attōḻik kuriya kaṭamiyām e-ṟu.
puṇarttal āṟṟal - kūṭumāṟu ceytal.
avaḷ eṉṟacuṭṭu mēli nūṟpāviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa tōḻiyi.
talimakaṉukkuk kuṟi nērntatōḻi, ivaḷ kūṭṭamuṇmi uṇarntāḷ pōṉṟu kuṟiyuṟukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟu talimakaṭkup pulaṉākāmi taṉ uḷḷattuṇarcciyi naṉkumaṟittut tali makaṉatu āṟṟāmik kuṟippiṉi maṭṭum talimakaṭku aṟi vuṟuttut talimakaḷik kuṟinayappikkum vaṇṇam ceytu talivaṉatu kuṟi muṭikkavallaḷ eṉpataṉi aṟivuṟuttuvatu.
		uḷḷat tuṇarcci teḷḷitiṟ karantu
kiḻavōḷ tēettuk kuṟiyuṟūum uḷavē
kuṟippaṟi vuṟūuṅ kāli yāṉa	(10)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalākum.
40.	kuṟiyeṉap paṭuva taraviṉum pakaliṉum
aṟiyak kiḷanta āṟṟa teṉpa.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu, mēl kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatākum eṉṟār, ataṟku ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟi eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu1 iraviṉāṉum pakaliṉāṉum iruvarum aṟiyac collappaṭṭa iṭatti yuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, iraviṟkuṟi, pakaṟkuṟi eṉ iruvakippaṭum eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.	(40)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭṭukiṉṟavaṭkuk kūṭutaṟkuriya kālamum iṭaṉuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyeṉap paṭuvatu - kuṟiyeṉṟu collap paṭuvatu; iraviṉum pakaliṉum. iraviṉkaṇṇum pakaliṉ kaṇṇum; aṟiyat tōṉṟum āṟṟatu eṉpa - talivaṉun taliviyun tāṉum aṟiyumpaṭi tōṉṟum neṟiyiyuṭiya iṭam (e-ṟu).2
neṟiyeṉṟār avaṉ varutaṟkuriya neṟi iṭameṉṟaṟku. atuveṉṟu orumiyāṟ kūṟiṉār, talippeyvatōriṭameṉṉum potumipaṟṟi. iravu kaḷaviṟkuc ciṟattaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār. aṟiyat tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṟ ceṉṟu kāṭṭal vēṇṭā niṉṟu kāṭṭal vēṇṭumeṉak koḷka.3	(39)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉ talivi iruvarum maṟiviṟ kalantu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuriya kuṟiyiṭamāvatu ituveṉak kūṟakiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) kuṟi eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu iravilum pakalilum talivaṉ talivi iruvarum tammuḷ aṟiyac collappaṭṭa vaḻi yiṉiyuṭiya iṭam eṉpar āciriyar e - ṟu.
āṟṟatu - vaḻiyiṉiyuṭiyatu : kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu. īṇṭu āṟu eṉṟatu kuṟiyiṭatti eḷitiṟ ceṉṟu cērtaṟkuriya vaḻiyiṉi. ittakiya vaḻiyamiyāta iṭam etuvum kuṟiyākātu eṉpār āṟṟatu eṉṟār. iravilum pakalilum eṉavē kaḷavoḻukkattiṟ kātalar iruvarum tammuṭ kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuriya vakiyiṟ kuṟitta iṭam iravukkuṟi pakaṟkuṟi eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉpatām. ittolkāppiya nūṟpāvi aṭiyoṟṟi yamintatu,
		kuṟiyeṉap paṭuva tiraviṉum pakaliṉum
aṟiyak kiḷanta iṭameṉa moḻipa	(18)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalākum.
41.	iravuk kuṟiyē illakat1 tuḷḷum
maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkumvaḻi yatuvē
maṉiyakam pukāak kāli yāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu, iravukkuṟikku iṭamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iravukkuṟiyām iṭamē illakattuḷ maṉiyakam pukāviṭattukkaṇ maṉiyōr kiḷavikēṭkum aṇimittām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, maṉikkum eyiṟkum naṭuvaṇatōriṭam eṉṟu koḷḷap paṭum.	(41)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē iravukkuṟiyiṭam uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akamaṉip pukāk kāli āṉa iravukkuṟiyē uṇmaṉiyiṟ ceṉṟu kūṭutaṟku urittallāta muṟkālattu uṇṭāṉa iravukkuṟiyē:1
ēkāram, pirinili.
illakattuḷḷum maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatuvē ilvarippiṉuḷḷa tākiyum maṉiyōr kūṟiya kiḷavi kēṭkum, piṟamaṉi2 yiṭattatām (e-ṟu).
allakuṟippaṭṭataṉi oruvāṟṟāṉ uṇarttiya kālattu avaṉ atukēṭṭu āṟṟuvaṉeṉpatu karuti, maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatu eṉṟār; ēkāram īṟṟaci: eṉṟatu, iravuk kuṟi am muyaṟcikkālattu accanikaḻtaliṉ, akamaṉikkum puṟa matiṟkum naṭuvē puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉṟatām. akamaṉiyiṟ pukākkāliyeṉavē, iravukkuṟi aṅṅaṉañ cilanāḷ nikaḻnta piṉṉar. accamiṉṟi uṇmaṉiyiṟ ceṉṟu kūṭavum peṟu meṉpatuṅ kūṟiyatām.3
u-m:		añcilam poṭukki yañciṉaḷ vantu
tuñcūr yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ	(akam.198)
eṉavum,
		miṭiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉe ṉivaḷeṉa
aliyal vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi	(akam. 158)
eṉavum,
aṭṭi lōli toṭṭaṉi niṉmē	(naṟṟiṇi. 300)
eṉavum varuvaṉa piṟavum maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatu.
		uḷimāṉ tuppi ṉōṅkutiṉip perumpuṉattuk
kaḻutiṟ kāṉavaṉ piḻimakiḻntu vatinteṉa
uritta cantiṉ ūra liruṅkatuppu
aituvara lacivaḷi māṟṟak kipeyarā
oliyiyal vārmayir uḷariṉaḷ koṭicci
peruvari maruṅkiṟ kuṟiñci pāṭak
kuraluṅ koḷḷātu niliyiṉum peyarātu
paṭāap piṅkaṇ pāṭupeṟ ṟoyyeṉa
maṟampukaṉ maḻakaḷi ṟuṟaṅku nāṭaṉ
āra mārpiṉ aṇimiñi ṟārppat
tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ eḵkuṭi valattaṉ
kāvala raṟital ōmpip piyeṉa
vīḻāk katava maciyiṉaṉ pukutan
tuyaṅkupaṭa rakala muyaṅkit tōṇ maṇan
tiṉco laḷiip peyarntaṉaṉ tōḻi
iṉṟevaṉ kollō kaṇṭiku maṟṟavaṉ
nalkā miyiṉ ampa lāki
oruṅkuvan tuvakkum paṇpiṉ
iruñcū ḻōti oṇṇutaṟ pacappē.	(akam, 102)
itu maṉiyakam pukkatu.
talivi puṟattup pōkiṉṟāḷeṉac cevilikku ōr aiyam nikaḻntavaḻip piṉṉar maṉiyakattup puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉṟuṇarka.		(40)
āyvuri
iravukkuṟiyiṉ iyalpu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iravukkuṟiyāvatu, talimakaḷatu ilvarippiṉuḷ maṉiyiluḷḷōr kūṟum coṟkaḷ kēṭkum aṇimikkaṇ maṉippuṟattu amivatākum; maṉiyiṉ uḷḷiṭattut talivaṉ pukātanilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
iravukkuṟiyē eṉpataṉ ēkāram iruvakik kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉip pirittuk kūṟaliṉ piriniliyēkāram. maṉiyōrkiḷavi - vīṭṭiṉuḷḷē vāḻum talivi, tōḻi, cevili mutaliyōr tammuṭ pēcikkoḷḷum coṟkaḷ. kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukum talivaṉ talimakaḷatu ilvarippiṉuḷ maṉippuṟattaṉṟi maṉiyakattē pukutal iravuk kuṟiyākāteṉa vilakkuvār maṉiyakam pukāk kāliyāṉa eṉṟār. eṉavē ilvarippiṉuḷ puṟamatiṟku uḷḷum akamaṉiyiṉ puṟamum ākiya maṉip paṭappiyēiravukkuṟiyātaṟkuriya iṭamām eṉpatu āciriyar karuttātal naṉkupeṟappaṭum.
iravuk kuṟiyē ilvari yikavātu	(19)
eṉavum,
		iravu maṉiyikanta kuṟiyiṭat tallatu
kiḻavōṟ cērtal kiḻattik killi	(21)
eṉavum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷ ittolkāppiyac cūttirapporuḷi viritturikkum muṟiyil amintami kāṇka.
42.	pakaṟpuṇar kaḷaṉē puṟaṉeṉa moḻipa
avaḷaṟi vuṇara varuvaḻi yāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu pakaṟkuṟi yāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pakaṟkuṟiyākippuṇarumiṭam eyiṟpuṟaṉ1 eṉṟu colluvar; āṇṭun talimakaḷ aṟivuṟṟuvarum iṭaṉākal vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.	(42)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu muṟiyāṉē pakaṟkuṟi uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṉ aṟivu2 uṇaravaruvaḻiāṉa pakaṟpuṇarkaḷaṉē kaḷañcuṭṭiya talivi aṟintaviṭan talivaṉ uṇarumpaṭiyāka varuvatōriṭattu uṇṭāṉa pakaṟpuṇaruṅkuṟiyiṭatti; puṟaṉ eṉa moḻipa matiṟpuṟattēyeṉṟu kūṟuvar aciriyar (e-ṟu).
aṟivu: ākupeyar.
u-m:		puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉaṟ puṇarkuṟi vāytta
miṉṉē rōtiyeṉ ṟōḻikku
eṉavum,
		pūvē puṉṉiyan taṇpoḻil
vāvē teyya maṇantaṉi celaṟkē	(akam. 239)
eṉavum varuvaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka.	(41)
āyvuri
itu, pakaṟkuṟiyāvatu ituveṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pakaliṟ kātalar iruvarum taṉimiyiṉ kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuriya pakaṟkuṟiyākiya iṭam ūriṉ puṟam eṉpar āciriyar. atutāṉum talimakaḷ aṟivuṟṟu uṇarntu varum vaḻiyiṉiyuṭiyatām nilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
avaḷ eṉṟatu talimakaḷi. puṟam eṉṟatu, iḷamakaḷir puṟattē pōy viḷiyāṭutaṟkuriya poḻil cūḻnta ūrppuṟat tiṉi. talimakaḷ aṟivuṟṟu uṇarntu talivaṉit talippaṭutaṟ kuriya vaḻiyuṭaṉ kūṭiyaviṭamē pakaṟkuṟiyākak koḷḷattakkatu eṉpār. avaḷ aṟivu uṇara varuvaḻiyāṉa puṟamē pakaṟ puṇarkaḷaṉ eṉṟār āciriyar. pakaṟkuṟitāṉē yikappiṉum variyār (20) eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaṟ cūttiram pakaṟ puṇarkaḷaṉē puṟam ātalip pulappaṭuttuvatākum.
43.	allakuṟip paṭutalum avaḷvayiṉ urittē
avaṉkuṟi1 mayaṅkiya amivoṭu variṉē.
iḷampūraṇam
ituvumatu.
(i - ḷ.) allakuṟippaṭutalun talimakaṭku urittu : talivaṉ ceyta kuṟimayaṅkiya poruttattoṭu variṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
mayaṅkiya amivu āvatu---avaṉ ceyyum kuṟiyōṭami vuṭiyaṉa.	(43)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu tōḻi alla kuṟippaṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. iru vakik kuṟi piḻippākiyaviṭattum eṉpuḻit talivi alla kuṟippaṭutal kūṟiṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) avaṉ kuṟi - talivaṉ taṉvaravu aṟivikkuṅ karuvikaḷ; mayaṅkiya amivoṭu variṉ - avaṉ ceyaṟki yāṉaṉṟi iyaṟkivakiyāṉē nikaḻntu tōḻi mayaṅkiya amitiyōṭē varumāyiṉ; allakuṟip paṭutalum - kuṟiyiṭattuk kūṭṭuṅkāl avvallavākiya kuṟiyilē mayaṅkutalum; avaḷ vayiṉ urittu - at tōḻiyiṭattu urittu2 (e - ṟu).
veṟittal veṟiyāyiṉāṟpōlak kuṟittal kuṟiyāyiṟṟu. ak karuvi puṉalolippaṭuttal mutaliyaṉa.
u-m:		koṭumuṇ maṭaṟṟāḻik kūṉpuṟa vāṉpū
iṭiyu ḷiḻutoppat tōṉṟip---puṭiyelām
teyvam kamaḻum teḷikaṭaṟ ṟaṇcērppaṉ
ceytāṉ ṟeḷiyāk kuṟi.	(aintiṇi aim. 49)
iḵtu allakuṟippaṭṭami ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu.
		eṟicuṟā nīḷkaṭal ōta mulāva
neṟiyiṟāk koṭku nimirkaṭaṟṟaṇ cērppaṉ
aṟivuṟā viṉco laṇiyiḻiyāy niṉṉiṟ
ceṟivaṟā ceyta kuṟi	(tiṇi. aim. 43)
iḵtu avaṉmēṟ kuṟiceykiṉṟami talivikkuk kūṟiyatu,
		iṭumaṇa lekka rakaṉkāṉaṟ cērppaṉ
kaṭumāṉ maṇiyarava meṉṟu---koṭuṅkuḻi
puḷḷaravaṅ kēṭṭup peyarntāḷ ciṟukuṭiyar
uḷḷarava nāṇuva reṉṟu.	(ain. eḻu 59)
iḵtu ivaṉ iṉi āṇṭuvariṟ cuṟṟattār aṟiva reṉṟatu.
		vīḻpeyaṟ kaṅkuṉiṉ viḷiyōrtta voṭukkattāl
vāḻunāḷ ciṟantavaḷ varuntutōṭ ṭavaṟuṇṭō
tāḻceṟi kaṭuṅkāppiṟ ṟāymuṉṉar niṉcāral
ūḻuṟu kōṭalpō loḷivaḷi yukupavāl.	(kali. 48)
itu talivaṟkup piṟṟiñāṉṟu kūṟiyatu.
		aṉṉi vāḻiyō vaṉṉinam paṭappi
miṉṉeṉa nuṭaṅkiṭik kiṉṉiḻa lākiya
puṉṉi meṉkāy pōkuciṉi yiriya
āṭuvaḷi tūkkiya vaciviṟ kollo
teṇṇīrp poykiyuḷ vīḻnteṉa
eṇṇiṉi yurimō vuṇarkuval yāṉē.
itu tōḻi tāykkuk kūṟuvāḷāy allakuṟi aṟivittatu.
		maṇiniṟa neytal iruṅkaḻic cērppaṉ
aṇinala muṇṭakaṉṟā ṉeṉṟuko lempōl
tiṇimaṇa lekkarmē lōtam peyarat
tuṇimunnīr tuñcā tatu	(ain. eḻu. 60)
itu tōḻi illuḷiruntu ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu.
tiṅkaḷmēl vittuk kūṟuvaṉavum ōtattiṉmēl vittuk kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavuṅ koḷka.
		aravaḷi meṉṟō ḷaṉuṅkat tuṟaṅtu
karavala meṉṟārik kaṇṭa tiliyāl
iravelā niṉṟāyā līrṅkatirt tiṅkāḷ
		puṉṉi naṉippiṉum pūñciṉi tōyiṉum
piṉṉiruṅ kūntaleṉ ṟōḻi naṭiyokku
maṉṉi naṉiyāti vāḻi kaṭalōtam
eṉa varuvaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka. paṭutal etirppaṭāmiyi uṇarttiṟṟu.1 āṇṭut taṉmēl tavaṟēṟṟātu talivaṉ poḻutaṟintu vārāmiyiṉ mayaṅkiṟṟeṉṟu amivu tōṉṟaliṉ ami veṉṟār.2 atu,
		tāṉkuṟi vāyāt tappaṟkut
tāmpacan taṉaveṉa ṟaṭameṉ ṟōḷē	(kuṟun. 121)
eṉṟāṟpōla varum. itaṉ payaṉ talivi tuṉpan taṉatākat tuṉpuṟutalāyiṟṟu.	(42)
āyvuri
itu, talimakaḷ alla kuṟippaṭumāṟum ataṟkuriya kāraṇamum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ taṉṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷōṭu iyaṟki nikaḻccikaḷ ottut tōṉṟi mayaṅkiya niliyil iravukkuṟikkaṇ varuvāṉāyiṉ avaṉ ceyta kuṟiyallāta iyaṟki nikaḻccikaḷi avaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kuṟikaḷākap piṟaḻavuṇarntu kuṟiyiṭattuc ceṉṟu talivaṉik kāṇātu tirumputalum talimakaṭku urittu e-ṟu.
kuṟimayaṅkiya amivoṭu avaṉ variṉ alla kuṟippaṭu talum avaḷvayiṉ urittu eṉa iyiyum. puḷḷeḻupputal, puṉalolippaṭuttal mutalākat talivaṉ ceyyum aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷiyottup paṟavikaḷ vēṟṟiṉap paṟavikaṇṭu eḻutalum kāy mutaliya nīrniliyil tuṭumeṉa vīḻtalum mutaliya iyaṟki nikaḻccikaḷ mayaṅkit tōṉṟiya nilimikkaṇ avaṟṟit talivaṉ ceyta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷāka eṇṇik kuṟiyiṭattē ceṉṟu talivaṉik kāṇappeṟātu alla kuṟippaṭṭu mīḷutalum talimakaṭku uriyatākum eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉāl uṇarttappaṭum ceytiyākum.
		alla kuṟippaṭutalum avvayiṉ urittē
avaṉvara vaṟiyum kuṟippi ṉāṉa (17)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal ittolkāppiya nūṟpāvi aṭiyoṟṟiṉintami kāṇka.
44.	aṅkāṅ koḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭē
ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ oruciṟi yāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam:
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) avvavviṭat toḻukum oḻukkamuṅ talivi māṭṭu uṇṭu. ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya orupakkattu eṉṟavāṟu.
oruciṟi yeṉṟatu maṉattāṉum moḻiyāṉum mey yāṉum kaṟpuṭi makaḷir oḻukum oḻukkattiṉ maṉattāṉ oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu.1	(44)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu talivaṉum allakuṟiyāl varuntuvaṉeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ - taṉatu mikka talimip pāṭṭiṉālē poḻutaṟintu vārāmiyiṉ; oruciṟi āṉa āṅku tāṉ kuṟiceyvatōriṭattē taṉṉāṉaṉṟi iyaṟkiyāṉ uṇṭāṉa avvalla kuṟiyiṭattē; āṅku oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu- taliviyun tōḻiyun tuṉpuṟumāṟu pōlat talivaṉun tuṉpuṟṟu oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu e-ṟu.2
muṉṉar niṉṟa āṅku muṉṉiṟcūttirattu allakuṟiyic cuṭṭiṟṟu; piṉṉar niṉṟa āṅku uvamurupu.	(43)
āyvuri:
itu, mēṟkūṟiya iruvakik kuṟikaṭkum eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kātalar iruvaruḷḷattē uyarntu vaḷartaṟkuriya ciṟappiṉatākiya pēraṉpiṉip pēṇi vaḷarkkum kāvalmintaṉ taṉimiyiṭamākiya nilimikkaṇ avvappoḻutum iṭamumākiya cūḻnilikkēṟpa maṟinta oḻukkamākiya kaḷaviṟkūṭṭam nikaḻtalum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
iravukkuṟi, pakaṟkuṟi eṉat tammāṟ kuṟittuk koḷḷappaṭṭa kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, avvāṟu kuṟittuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kātalar iruvar taṉimiyiṟ kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟku ēṟṟa cūḻ niliyum pātukāppum uṭiyatāyt taṉṉiyalpil nērpaṭum iṭamum poḻutum kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻtaṟkuriya pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikaḷukkuriya kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷāka amitalum uṇṭu eṉpatu innūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum ciṟappu vitiyākum.
ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ oru ciṟiyāṉa āṅkāṅku oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu eṉa iyittup poruḷ koḷka. ōṅkiya ciṟappāvatu, kātalar iruvaratu uḷḷamum mēṉmēl uyarcci peṟutaṟkuriyatāy avviruvaruḷḷattē kiḷarntu tōṉṟum pēraṉpākum. oruciṟi - piṟar aṟiyāvāṟu pātukāvalaminta taṉiyiṭam. oruciṟi neñcōṭucāvuṅkāli (kaṟpiyal-4) eṉpuḻi oruciṟi eṉṉuñ col taṉittu eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkap peṟutal iṅku oppu nōkkiyuṇarattakuvatākum.
45.	maṟinta oḻukkat1 tōriyum nāḷun
tuṟanta oḻukkaṅ kiḻavaṟ killi.
iḷampūraṇam:
eṉṟatu, talivaṟku uriya iyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavoḻukkattumukūrttamum nāḷum tuṟantoḻukum oḻukkam talivaṟku illi eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉṟataṉāṉ, āṇṭu aṟattiṉ vaḻuviṉāṉallaṉ talivi māṭṭut, taliyaḷi kuṟitalāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.2	(45)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu talivaṟku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ōriyum nāḷun tuṟanta oḻukkam- tīya irāciyiṉ kaṇṇun tīya nāḷiṉkaṇṇuṅ kūṭṭattit tuṟanta oḻukkam1 kiḻavōṟku maṟinta oḻukkattu illi talivaṟkuk kaḷa voḻukkattiṉkaṇ illi; eṉavē kaṟpiṉkaṇuḷa (e-ṟu).
oḻukkamāvatu cīlamātaliṟ cīlaṅkāraṇattāl tuṟappatu tītākiya irāciyum nāḷumeṉpatu peṟṟām. nāḷāvatu avvirāci maṇṭila muḻuvatum kiḻatti tuṟanta oḻukkam  munnāḷallateṉa (122) muṟkūṟiṟṟu itaṉāṉ aṉpu mikuti kūṟiṉār. ïj‰F¥ ãuha¢á¤j« mªjz® KjÈa _t®¡F« c©ik ‘tªj F‰w« tÊbfl bthGfY«’ (bjhš.bgh.146) vd¡ f‰ãaÈ‰ TWg.(44)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ talimakaṉukkāvatōr kaṭamiyuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ulakattāraṟiyātu kātalar iruvarum maṟin toḻukum oḻukalāṟākiya kaḷavoḻukkam kāraṇamākat tāṉ mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkuriya viḷiyāṭṭum viḻāvum ākiya ciṟappuṭiya nikaḻccikaḷi nīṅkiyoḻukum oḻukalāṟu talivaṉukku illi e-ṟu.
maṟinta oḻukkam eṉṟatu kaḷavoḻukkattiṉi. ōri eṉṉuñ col. viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum taṉittamiḻc collākum. kōtiyāyamoṭu ōritaḻīi (akam 49) eṉavum. ōriyāyam (akam.219) (kuṟun.48) eṉavum, viḷiyāṭāyattu ōriyāṭātu (naṟṟiṇi 2) eṉavum ōri yeṉṉuñ col viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷil caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ payiṉṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. innūṟpāvil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa ōriyeṉṉuñ collum viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷilēyē āḷappeṟṟuḷḷami kūrntuṇartaṟkuriyatākum. nāḷ-tiruviḻā, māyōṉmēyōṇanaṉṉāḷ (maturik kāñci-591) eṉavum, ātiri nāḷ kāṇātē pōtiyōpūmpāvāy (tēvāram 3-47-4) eṉavum, nāḷ eṉpatu tiruviḻā eṉṉum poruḷiṟ payila vaḻaṅkutal iṅku oppunōkkaṟpālatākum.
talivaṉ tāṉ virumpi mēṟkoṇṭoḻukum kaḷa veḻukkam kāraṇamākat tāṉ taṉ tōḻarkaḷuṭaṉ tavaṟātu kalantu koḷḷutaṟkuriya ciṟappuṭiya viḷiyāṭṭiṉiyum ūrmakkaḷ palarum kāṇat tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭu nikaḻttutaṟkuriya tiruviḻāc ceyalmuṟikaḷiyum kiviṭṭu vilakiyoḻuku vāṉāyiṉ, annikaḻccikaḷil avaṉ kalantu koḷḷāmipaṟṟi avaṉik kuṟittup palarum viṉavāniṟka, atu kāraṇamāka avaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāmātaliṉ. kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukuntalivaṉ ōriyum nāḷum ākiya ivaṟṟi nīṅki yoḻukutal kūṭātu eṉat talivaṉukkuriya iṉṟiyāmiyāta kaṭamiyiṉi vaṟpuṟuttum niliyil amintatu innūṟpā vākum.
46.	āṟiṉa tarumi1yum aḻivum accamum
ūṟum uḷappaṭa ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḷampūraṇam
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṟiyiṉatu arumiyum maṉaṉ aḻivum añcutalum iṭiyūṟum talivaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻā eṉṟavāṟu.	(46)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
ituvun talivaṟku illaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) uḷappaṭa nilavum iruḷum pakivarum pōlvaṉa paṟṟic celavaḻuṅkutal uḷappaṭa; āṟiṉatu arumiyum neṟiyiṉatu arumi niṉintu kūṭṭa nikaḻntavaḻik kūṟutalum; aḻivum - kuṟinta maṉattaṉātalum; accamum- pāmpum vilaṅkum pōlvaṉa naliyumeṉṟu añcutalum; ūṟum- ak karumattiṟku iṭiyūṟu uḷavāṅkoleṉṟu aḻuṅkutalum; ataṉōr aṉṉa- kiḻavaṟku illi (e-ṟu.)
kiḻavaṟkilliyeṉavē kiḻattikkun tōḻikkum uḷa vāyiṟṟu.1 avi muṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuṭ kāṇka.	(45)
āyvuri
ituvum talimakaṉukkuriyatōr iyalpuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṟiyiṉatu arumiyum maṉaṉ aḻivum accamum iṭiyūṟukaḷum uṭpaṭa uḷavākun taṭikaḷ kāraṇamākat tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa kaḷavoḻukkattiṉit tavirtalum talivaṉukku illi e-ṟu.
āṟṟiṉatu arumiyāvatu, naṭattaṟkiyalātavāṟu ēṟṟiḻivum pālum muḷḷum putarum kaṭum puṉalāṟum ceṟintaviruḷum uṭiya ariyavaḻiyiṉatu ātal. aḻivu-maṉaṉ aḻivu; cōmpal, accam ---āṭavaṉukku ēlāta accavuṇarvu. ūṟu - koṭiya vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟāl vaḻiyiṭiyē yuḷavām iṭiyūṟukaḷ. uḷappaṭa- ivi kāraṇamāka. āṟṟiṉatu arumiyum aḻivum accamum ūṟum kāraṇamāka maṟinta oḻukkattiṉit tuṟantu talimakaḷik kaḷaviṟ kāṇātu tavirtal talivaṉukku illi eṉpār, ataṉōraṟṟē eṉṟār. kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukum talivaṉ āṟṟiṉatu arumi mutaliya taṭikaḷi eṇṇittaṉ āruyirk kātaliyākiya taliviyik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvum kātaṟ kēṇmi yiṉit tavirvāṉa allaṉ eṉa avaṉatu kātaṟ kaṭamiyiṉi vaṟpuṟuttum muṟiyil amintatu innūṟpāvākum.
47.	tantiyun taṉṉiyum1 muṉṉattiṉ uṇarpa.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu, tantiyum, taṉṉiyarum kaḷavu uṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tantiyarum taṉṉiyarum kuṟippiṉ uṇarpa eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, kūṟṟiṉāṉ urikkappeṟār eṉṟavāṟām.	(47)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu tantiyun taṉṉiyuṅ kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) tantiyun taṉṉiyum oruvar kūṟakkoḷḷātu uyttuk koṇṭuṇarvar2 (e - ṟu).
naṟṟāy aṟattoṭu niṉṟa vaḻiyum.
		iruvarkaṇ kuṟṟamu milliyā leṉṟu
terumantu cāyttār tali	(kali.39)
eṉṟaliṉ, muṉṉar nikaḻnta vekuṭci nīṅki uyttukkoṇṭu uṇarntārāyiṟṟu.	(46)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavoḻukkam taliviyiṉ tanti taṉṉiyarkkup pulaṉāmāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) taliviyiṉ tantiyum tamiyaṉmārum kaḷa voḻukkattiṉik kuṟippiṉāl uṇarvar e-ṟu.
 muṉṉam - kuṟippu. eṉavē ivarkku naṟṟāy aṟattoṭu niṟkuṅkāli uriyiṉāl veḷippaṭak kūṟavēṇṭiya iṉṟi yamiyāmi illiyeṉpatām.
		tanti taṉṉiyar āyiru vīṟṟum
muṉṉam allatu kūṟṟavaṉ illi
eṉpatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal.
48.	tāy aṟivuṟutal ceviliyō ṭokkum.
iḷampūraṇam
 eṉṟatu, naṟṟāykku uriyatoru marapuṇarttul nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam aṟivuṟutal ceviliyō ṭokkum eṉṟavāṟu. cevili kavaluntuṇik kavalutalallatu tantiyiyum taṉṉiyaṉmāriyum pōla vekuṭalilaḷ1 eṉṟavāṟu. avar vekuḷvarō eṉiṉ,
kāmar kaṭumpuṉal eṉat toṭaṅkum kalittokip pāṭṭiṉuḷ,
		aṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉik kaṇṭu tiṟappaṭa
eṉṉiyark kuytturittāḷ yāy	(kalit. 39)
eṉat tāyvekuḷāmi kāṇappaṭṭatu.
		avarum terikaṇi nōkkic cilinōkkik kaṇcēntu
orupaka lellām urutteḻun tāṟi	(kalit. 39)
eṉṟataṉāṉ vekuṭci peṟṟām.	(48)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu tanti taṉṉikku naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarttu māṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tāy aṟivuṟutal - naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam uṇ ṭeṉṟu aṟinta aṟivu tantikkun taṉṉikkuñ ceṉṟu uṟuntaṉmi;1 ceviliyōṭu okkum-cevili naṟṟāykku aṟat toṭu niṉṟu uṇarttiya taṉmiyōṭu okkum (e-ṟu).
eṉṟatu, cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟāṟpōla, naṟṟāyun tantikkun taṉṉikkum aṟattoṭu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. atu,
		eṉavāṅ, kaṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉik kaṇṭu tiṟappaṭa
veṉṉiyark kuytturittāṉ yāy	(kali. 39)
eṉpataṉāṉ uṇarka.
iṉi itaṟku naṟṟāyuñ cevili uṇarntāṟpōla uṇaru meṉṟu poruḷ kūṟil tāykkum variyār (tol. po. 116) eṉṉuñ cūttiram vēṇṭāvām.	(47)
āyvuri
itu naṟṟāy uṇarumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) naṟṟāy talimakaḷatu kaḷavoḻukkattiṉi aṟital ceviliyuṇarum muṟimiyiṉōṭu okkum e-ṟu.
avaruḷ, tāyaṟivuṟutaliṉ ēṉōrum aṟipa	(23)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal tāyvaḻiyākavē tanti taṉṉiyar uṇarvar eṉpataṉi vaṟpuṟuttuvatākum.
49.	ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavuveḷip paṭuttaliṉ
aṅkataṉ mutalvaṉ kiḻava ṉākum.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉṟatu kaḷavu veḷippaṭuppār avar eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ampal eṉpatu mukiḻttal. aḵtu oruvaroruvar mukakkuṟippiṉāṟ ṟōṟṟuvittal. alarāvatu collutal.2 ataṉāṉē ivi iraṇṭum kaḷaviṉi veḷippaṭuttaliṉ ataṟkuk kāraṇamāvāṉ talivaṉ eṉṟavāṟu. eṉṉi, avaṉi yaṟin tuḻiyallatu ivi nikaḻāmiyiṉ. talivi vēṟupāṭṭāṉ ākātō eṉiṉ. āṇṭu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉa aiya nikaḻtalallatu. tuṇivu piṟavātām eṉṟu koḷka.	(46)
nacciṉākkiṉiyam
itu kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaṟku nimittamāvāṉ talimakaṉeṉ kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ mukiḻttalum palaraṟiyac col nikaḻttaluṅ kaḷavoḻukkattiṉi veḷippaṭuttalāṉ; aṅkataṉ mutalvaṉ1 kiḻavaṉ ākum - av-viṭattu avveḷippaṭi nikaḻttutaṟku nimittamāyiṉāṉ talimakaṉām (e-ṟu).
talivaṉi aṟintapiṉ allatu muṟkūṟiya aiyam nikaḻāmiyiṉ taliviruttam nimittamākā. āṇṭu aiyam nikaḻtalaṉṟit tuṇivu tōṉṟāmiyiṉ. varivu nīṭṭippōṉun, talivi tamarkkuk kūṟi veḷippaṭuppōṉun talivaṉē eṉṟuṇarka. atu,
		nīrolit taṉṉa pēer
alarnamak koḻiya aḻappirin tōrē
	(akam. 211)
eṉavum,
		neṟiyaṟi ceṟikuṟi puritiri paṟiyā
vaṟivaṉi muntuṟīi	(kali. 39)
eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavum piṟavum veḷippaṭiyāmāṟṟāṟ kaṇṭuṇarka.	(48)
āyvuri
itu, kaḷavu veḷippaṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamāvār ivar eṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iraviṉum pakaliṉum vantucellum talivaṉik kaṇṭu pulappaṭuttum ampalum, iṉṉāṉōṭu iṉṉāḷiṭiyatu naṭpu eṉac collāl viritturippatākiya alarum kātalar iruvaratu kaḷavoḻukkattiṉip palarum aṟiya veḷippaṭuttalāl attakiya kaḷavu veḷippaṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamāvāṉ talivaṉē e-ṟu.
ampāl-arumpāl; makaḷiratu mukak kuṟippiṉāl kaḷa voḻukkam arumpu pōṉṟu ciṟitu veḷippaṭutal: alar-alarpōṉṟu kūṟṟiṉāl virintu veḷippaṭutal. aṅku ataṉ mutalvaṉ anniliyil akkaḷaviṉ veḷippāṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇa māvāṉ mutal-kāraṇam. ampalum alarum tōṉṟiya piṉṉarallatu immaṟi veḷippaṭātātaliṉ. ikkaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkut talivaṉē kāraṇamāvāṉ eṉpatu karuttu.
ampalum alarum kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ
eṉavarum it tolkāppiyat toṭari.
ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavu (22)
eṉavum,
veḷippaṭṭa piṉṟiyum uriyakiḷavi (23)
eṉavum iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷāka iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalāciriyar amittuk koṇṭuḷḷār.
50.	veḷippaṭa varitalpaṭāmi varital eṉṟu
āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa varital āṟē.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, variyum pakuti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ varitalum kaḷavu veḷip paṭāmi varitalum eṉa avviraṇṭeṉṟu colluvar variyum neṟi eṉṟavāṟu.1
itaṉāl colliyatu iruvāṟṟāṉum aṟaṉ iḻukkā teṉṟavāṟu. aḵtu iḻukkātavāṟu varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ uṇarka.	(50)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
(i - ḷ.) veḷippaṭa varital- muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉṉar varintu kōṭal; paṭāmi varital akkaḷavu veḷippaṭuvataṉ muṉṉar varintukōṭal ; eṉṟu ā iraṇṭu eṉpa varital āṟē - eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭē eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar varintukoḷḷum vaḻiyi (e-ṟu).
		cēyuyar veṟpaṉum vantaṉaṉ
pūveḻil uṇkaṇum polikamā iṉiyē.	(kali. 39)
itu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ varivunikaḻntatu.
		kollip puṉatta akilcumantu kaṟpāyntu
vāṉiṉ aruvi tatumpak kaviṉiya
nāṭa ṉayaṉuṭiya ṉeṉpataṉāṉ nīppiṉum
vāṭaṉ maṟantaṉa tōḷ.	(aintiṇi eḻu. 2)
itu veḷippaṭāmal varivu nikaḻntatu.
		emmaṉi muntuṟat tarumō
taṉmaṉi yuykkumō yātavaṉ kuṟippē	(akam. 195)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa veḷippaṭuvataṉmuṉṉark koṇṭu talik kaḻintuḻik koṭuppōriṉṟiyuṅ karaṇam nikaḻntamiyiṉ, atuvum veḷippaṭāmal varintām.	(46)
āyvuri
itu, talivaṉ taliviyi varintu koḷḷum pakuti ittuṇi eṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉart taliviyi maṇantu koḷḷutalum atu veḷippaṭutaṟku muṉrē maṇantu koḷḷutalum eṉa maṇantu koḷḷum neṟi iru tiṟappaṭum e - ṟu.
innūṟpāvi viritturikkum muṟiyilamintatu,
		kaḷavu veḷippaṭā muṉṉuṟa varital
kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṟi varitaleṉa
āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa varita lāṟē (24)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaṟ cūttiramākum.
51.	veḷippaṭi tāṉē kaṟpiṉo ṭoppiṉum
ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟuporu ḷāka
variyātu pirital kiḻavōṟ killi.
iḷampūraṇam
itu, talivaṟku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭutal kaṟpiṉōṭoppiṉum mēṟcollappaṭṭamūṉṟu poruḷāka variyātu pirital kiḻa vōṟku illi eṉṟavāṟu.1
aviyāvaṉa muṟkūṟiya ōtaṟpirivum, pakivayiṟpirivum, tūtiṟ pirivum. eṉavē poruḷvayiṟ piritalum vēntarkkuṟṟuḻippiri talum kāvaṟpiritalum nikaḻappeṟum eṉṟavāṟām.2	(51)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam
itu mutaṟkūṟiya varivu nikaḻttātu piriyum iṭam itu veṉavum piriyalākā iṭam ituveṉavuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) veḷippaṭitāṉē kaṟpiṉoṭu oppiṉum-muṟkūṟiya veḷippaṭitāṉē kaṟpiṉuḷ talivi urimi ciṟantāṅku arumi ciṟantu kaṟpōṭotta tāyiṉum; ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟu1 poruḷāka - muṟkūṟiya ōtal paki tūteṉṟa mūṉṟum nimittamāka; variyātu pirital kiḻavōṟku illi-variviṭivittup pirital talimakaṟkilli (e-ṟu).2
mūṉṟumeṉa muṟṟummi koṭātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ēṉip pirivukaḷiṉ variyātu piriyappeṟum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. avi varitaṟkup poruḷvayiṟ piritalum, vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip piritaluṅ, kāvaṟkup piritalumeṉa mūṉṟumām.
u-m:		poṉṉaṭarn taṉṉa voḷḷiṇarc ceruntip
paṉmalar vēynta nalampeṟu kōtiyaṉ
tiṇimaṇa laṭikari yalava ṉāṭṭi
aciyiṉa ḷirunta vāytoṭik kuṟumakaḷ
nalañcāl viḻupporuḷ kalaniṟi koṭuppiṉum
peṟalaruṅ kuriya ḷāyi ṉaṟanterintu
nāmuṟi tēe marūup peyarn tavaṉo
ṭirunīrc cērppi ṉuppuṭaṉu ḻantum
perunīrk kuṭṭam puṇiyoṭu pukkum
paṭuttaṉam paṇintaṉa maṭuttaṉa miruntapiṟ
ṟarukuvaṉ kollō tāṉē viritirik
kaṇṭiraṇ muttaṅ koṇṭu ñāṅkart
tēṉimir taṇkarip pakukkuṅ
kāṉalam peruntuṟip paratavaṉ namakkē.	(akam. 280)
itaṉuḷ īṇṭuḷḷa poruḷ koṭuttāṟ peṟal ariya ḷāyiṉ taṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭāl tanti taruvaṉō? atu namak karitākaliṉ iṉṉum poruḷ nām mikat tēṭivantu varitu meṉap poruḷvayiṟ piriyak karutiyavāṟu kāṇka.
		pūṅkoṭi maruṅkuṟ polampū ṇōyē
vēntu viṉimuṭittu vantaṉar
kāntaṇ melviraṟ kaviyiṉi niṉimē.
itu vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip pirintāṉ varivu malintami tōḻi kūṟiyatu ēṉiya vantuḻik kāṇka. ōtutaṟku ēvuvār iru mutu kuravarātaliṉ, avar variyāmaṟ pirikaveṉṟār. pakiveṉṟu tiṟikōṭaṟkup piriyuṅkāl, aṉpuṟu kiḻatti tuṉpuṟṟiruppa variyātu piritaliṉṟu. itu tūtiṟkum okkum maṟiveḷippaṭutal (499) kaṟpeṉṟu ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kūṟutaliṉ, itaṉi ivvōttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vittār. kaṟpiṉō ṭoppiṉum piriviṉṟeṉavē. kaṟpiṟkāyiṟ pirivu variviṉṟāyiṟṟu.	(50)
āyvuri
itu, talivaṉukku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippāṭu kaṟpiyal vāḻkkiyōṭu oppa tāyiṉum muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa ōtal, paki, tūtu eṉṉum im mūṉṟum kāraṇamākat taliviyi maṇantu koḷḷātu neṭṭiṭip (neṭuntūram) pirintu cellutal taviṉukku uriya ceyalātal illi e-ṟu.
ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟu eṉpa : ōtal pakiyē tūtu ivi pirivē (tol. akat. 27) eṉa muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa mūvakip pirivukaḷ. ñāṅkar -muṉ. poruḷ - kāraṇam. ōtal, paki, tūtu eṉṉum immūṉṟum kāraṇamākat talimakaḷi variyātu pirital illi; eṉavē variviṭivittup poruḷ vayiṟ piritalum vēntaṟku uṟutuṇiyākap piritalum nāṭu kāttaṟkup piritalum talimakaṉukku uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟām.
kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉ variyum kālattu ivaṟkuriyavaḷ ivaḷ eṉ ellārāṉum muṉṉamē aṟiyappeṟṟamiyāl av- viruvarum varitaṟku muṉṉarē kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭavarāyiṉamiyiṉ avartam vāḻkki kaṟpiyal vāḻkkiyoṭu ottatāyiṟṟu eṉpār, veḷippaṭitāṉē kaṟpiṉōṭoppiṉum eṉṟār āciriyar. ittoṭari,
		veḷippaṭi tāṉē kaṟpiṉō ṭoppiṉum
tantitāyē taṉṉiya reṉṟāṅ
kaṉṉava raṟiyap paṇpā kummē	(26)
eṉavarum nūṟpāvil iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalāciriyar ivvāṟē yeṭuttāṇṭuḷḷami tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa kaḷaviyaliyum kaṟpiyaliyum aṭiyoṟṟiyeḻuntatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal eṉṉum uṇmiyi naṉku pulappaṭuttuvatākum.

1.	itu kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarttiṉamiyāl kaḷaviyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
2.	kaḷaveṉpatu aṟam aṉmiyiṉ īṇṭu urikkiṉṟataṉāl payaṉ iṉṟām eṉil eṉa iyittup poruḷ kāṇka.
3.	kaḷaveṉum coṟkēṭṭuk kaḷavu tīteṉpatūum, kāmam eṉṉum coṟkēṭṭuk kāmam tīteṉpatūum aṉṟu; maṟṟavi nallavāmāṟum uṇṭu eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluriyiṉi aṭiyoṟṟiyamintatu ivvurip pakutiyākum.
4.	iṉṉataṉṟi eṉpataṉi, iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi eṉat tiruttuka.
5.	aṟanili vaḻāmaṉṟalāl eṉpatu muṉpatippiluḷḷa pāṭam; aṟanili vaḻāmaṉiṟṟalāl eṉa iṅkut tiruttap peṟṟatu.
1.	orutali vēṭkiyāvatu, oruvaṉ oruttiyeṉṉum irupālaruḷ oru variṭattē maṭṭum tōṉṟum viruppam. iḵtu orutalik kāmam eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟum.
kikkiḷi yuṭiya torutalik kāmam	(3)
eṉpatu nampiyakap poruḷ.
2.	oppil kūṭṭamāvatu, oruvaṉ oruttiyeṉṉum iruvariṭiyē aṉpiṉṟi nikaḻum poruttamillāta kūṭṭuṟavu. itu poruntāk kāmam eṉappaṭum.
peruntiṇi yeṉpatu poruntāk kāmam	(4)
eṉpatu nampiyakap poruḷ.
3.	aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyoḻukalāṟṟiṉ potu vilakkaṇam uṇarttum akattiṇiyiyaliṉpiṉ ataṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷākiya kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉṉum oḻukalāṟukaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam uṇarttum kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal eṉṉum iyalkaḷi vittal muṟiyāyiṉum, aṅṅaṉam vippiṉ, veṭcitāṉē kuṟiñciyatu puṟaṉē eṉpatu mutalāka akattiṇi ēḻaṉōṭum puṟattiṇi ēḻaṉiyum māṭṭeṟintu kūṟum puṟattiṇi yilakkaṇam tiṟappaṭap pulaṉākātavāṟu kaḷaviyalum kaṟpiyalum iṭippaṭṭuk kiṭakkumātalāl, akattiṇiyiyaliṉ piṉ akattoṭu toṭarpuṭiya puṟattiṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟum puṟattiṇiyiyali vittu, akattiṇiyiṉ ciṟappilakkaṇamuṇarttum kaḷaviyali ataṉpiṉ vittār āciriyar eṉa mēli iyalōṭu ivviyalukkuḷḷa toṭarpiṉi viḷakkuvatu ivvurip pakutiyākum.
4.	iṭantalippāṭum. pā. vē.
5.	aṟinta cārpoṭu. pā. vē.
