1.	kavarttu niṉṟa aṟivāvatu, nām avari virumpumāṟupōla avarum nammi virumpuvārō virumpamāṭṭārō? eṉak kātalar iruvaruḷḷattum aiyaniliyil irutalippaṭṭu nikaḻum aṟivu. iṅṅaṉam nikaḻum iruvaratu aṟiviṉiyum oruppaṭuttalāvatu, nām avarik kātalippatu pōṉṟē avarum nammik kātalikkiṉṟār eṉṉum kātaṟ kuṟippiṉi iruvaratu aṟivum teḷintu uṭaṉpaṭumāṟu ceytal.
2.	kūṭṭiyurittalāvatu, uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi ataṟkuk kāraṇamāy nikaḻum vēṭkiyuṭaṉ cērttuṇarttal, ittakiya kāmak kuṟippuriyiṉip pulappaṭuttaṟkuriya karuviyāka amintaṉa talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvaruṭiya kaṇkaḷum eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉāl aṟivuṟuttappeṟum nuṇporuḷākum. innuṭpattiṉi eḷiya coṟkaḷāl teḷivākap pulappaṭuttum muṟiyil iḷampūraṇaruri amintiruttal uṇarntu makiḻattakuvatākum.
1.	iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṟ kātalar iruvariṭiyē mutaṟkaṇ nikaḻum kāṭci, oruvar uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi maṟṟavar uṇarntu koḷḷāta nilimikkaṇ viruppuṟṟuk kāṇum orumaruṅku paṟṟiya kēṇmiyātaliṉ, aḵtu orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum. avvāṟaṉṟik kātalar iruvar kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṉi nōkku otta niliyil nikaḻum kāṭci, avviruvarum meyyuṟu puṇarcci peṟutaṟku nimittamākip piṉṉar nikaḻtaliṉ,/atu vaḻinilik kāṭci eṉappaṭum.
2.	aṟivu uṭampaṭuttaṟku naṭṭam iraṇṭum kūṭṭi urikkum eṉavum, kuṟippuriyum nāṭṭam iraṇṭum ākum eṉavum iru toṭarāka iyittup poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟuḷ, mutal toṭar talivaṉatu nāṭṭattiṉ ceyaliyum, iraṇṭān toṭar talimakaḷatu nāṭṭattiṉ ceyaliyum kuṟittaṉa nāṭṭam iraṇṭum eṉṟa toṭar talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum kūṭṭip poruḷ urikkappaṭṭatu.
aṟivu uṭampaṭuttalāvatu, talivaṉatu aṟivālē taliviyik kūṭṭattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭac ceytal; talivaṉ taṉṉuṭiya nōkkam iraṇṭāṉum kūṭṭivārtti collum; avvēṭki kaṇṭu talivi taṉatu vēṭkiyip pulappaṭuttik kūṟuṅ kuṟippuriyum taṉṉuṭiya nōkkam iraṇṭiṉālumē ām eṉpatu innūṟpāvukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟum poruḷākum. kuṟippuriyum eṉa ummi virittup poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kātalar iruvaruḷ oruvaratu nōkku maṟṟavaratu nōkkiṉi ūṉṟi nōkkutaliṉ avviruvar nōkkiṉiyum nāṭṭam eṉṟa collāṟ kuṟittār āciriyar. nāṭṭam--nāṭṭutal; ūṉṟutal.
1.	īṇṭu, kuṟippu eṉṟatu, oruvar tam uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭa kuṟippiṉi.
2.	kuṟittatu eṉṟatu, piṟar tam uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭa kuṟippiṉi.
3.	kuṟippē kuṟittatu koḷḷutalāvatu, kuṟippiṟ kuṟippuṇartalākum. 
4.	āṅku avi eṉṟatu, avviṭattukkaṇṇiṉāṟ kūṭṭiyurikkappaṭum kuṟippuriyiṉi kuṟippaṟital eṉpatu akattiṟkum puṟattiṟkum potuvātaliṉ kuṟippaṟital eṉpataṉip poruṭpālilum kāmattup pālilum iruvēṟu atikāraṅkaḷāka amittār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
muṉpakkattoṭarcci
		kuṟippiṟ kuṟippuṇarā vāyiṉ uṟuppiṉuḷ
eṉṉa payattavō kaṇ.	(kuṟaḷ. 705)
		pakimiyuṅ kēṇmiyuṅ kaṇṇurikkuṅ kaṇṇiṉ
vakimi yuṇarvārp peṟiṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 709)
eṉap poruṭpālilum,
		kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṉi nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ
eṉṉa payaṉu mila.	(1110)
eṉak kāmattup pālilum aminta kuṟaṭpākkaḷ,
		nāṭṭa miraṇṭum aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yurikkuṅ kuṟippuri yākum	(tol-kaḷa-5)
		āṅkavi nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar	(tol-kaḷa-6)
eṉa varum ittolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷi aṭiyoṟṟiyamintaṉavākum.
1.	innūṟpāvil varum avi eṉṉum cuṭṭu, kaṇṇiṉāl varum kuṟippuriyiṉik cuṭṭiyateṉak koṇṭār iḷampūraṇar. avi eṉpatu pukumukam purital mutalāka irukiyumeṭuttal īṟākap piṉṉar meyp pāṭṭiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum meyppāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷ poṟinutal viyarttal mutaliya patiṉoṉṟiṉiyum cuṭṭiyateṉak koṇṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iḷampūraṇar kūṟum kaṇṇiṉāl varum kuṟippuri mēlic cūttirattil nāṭṭamiraṇṭum kūṭṭiyurikkum kuṟippuri eṉa viḷakkap peṟṟamiyiṉ ataṉiyē ic cūttiramum cuṭṭiyateṉak koḷḷutal kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭumātalāṉum, iṭam variyātu nūṟpāvil varum avi eṉṉum cuṭṭu, ataṉ aṇittāka muṉṉōpiṉṉōvuḷḷavaṟṟic cuṭṭuva taṉṟi ivviyaliṉ mūṉṟām iyalākiya meyppāṭṭiyalil viritturikkappaṭum paṉṉiraṇṭu meyppāṭukaḷuḷ patiṉoṉṟiṉi maṭṭum varintu kūṭṭutal iyalātātalāṉum uyiciriyarkaḷ iruvarum kūṟum urikaḷ tolkāppiyaṉār karuttiṉip pulappaṭuttuvaṉavāka amiyavilli.
1.	añcuvatañcutalākiya perumiyum maṉattik ceṉṟaviṭattāṟ cella viṭātu naṉṟiṉpāṟ celuttum nallaṟivum ākiya iviyiraṇṭum āṇmakaṉuk kuriya ciṟappiyalpākum.
2.	ittuṇiyum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa uḷḷappuṇarccikkēyuriyavāy meyyuṟu puṇarccikku nimittamāvaṉavēyaṉṟi meyyuṟu puṇarccikku uriyaṉavē eṉak koḷḷutal poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṉṟavilli.
1.	talivikku meyyuṟu puṇarcci naṭakkum vēṭki nikaḻāmikkuk kāraṇam ivviyalil aṭuttu varum nūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum.
2.	iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvōr iḷampūraṇar. iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutalākac collappaṭṭa nāṉkum uḷḷappuṇarccikkum ataṉvaḻi nikaḻum meyyuṟu puṇarccikkum potuvākiya iṭaṅkaḷātaliṉ, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyilēyē meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻtal vēṇṭumeṉṉum yāppuṟavilliyeṉka.
3.	muntuṟutal - muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟutal; uḷḷattut tōṉṟiya vēṭkiyi mēleḻavoṭṭatu piṟpaṭuttit tāṉē muṟpaṭṭu niṟṟal.
muntuṟutta eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar koṇṭa pāṭam.    
4.	niccamum - nāḷtōṟum - nittam niccam eṉat (takarattiṟkuc cakaram pōliyāy vantatu - nittam-nāḷ.
1.	muntuṟutta - mutaliyaṉa. muntuṟutal eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar koṇṭa pāṭam.
 2.	niccamum - nittamum; ennāḷum; takara cakarap pōli.
3. ikkantaruvam uḷḷap puṇarcciyākiya kantaruvam.
1.	akatti - uṇarvunili. kāṭci vikaṟpamākiya aiyamum tuṇivum mutalavatti eṉak koṇṭu, ataṉuṭaṉ vēṭki mutaliya oṉpatiṉiyum kūṭṭa avatti pattum iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum, cātal pattām avatti eṉpatum, vēṭki mutaliya eṭṭum kaḷavu nikaḻtaṟkuk kāraṇamām eṉpatum, ivvuṇarvu nilikaḷ talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvarkkum okkum eṉpatum iḷampūraṇar karuttākum.
1.	oru taṉmi allatu uṟuti eṉṟa poruḷil innūṟpāvil vantuḷḷa orutali eṉpataṉi orutali uḷḷutal eṉap piṉvarum uḷḷutaṟku aṭiyākki, iṭiviṭātu niṉittal eṉap poruḷ koṇṭār iḷampūraṇar. itaṉi iṭinili viḷakkākak koṇṭu orutali vēṭki eṉa muṉṉuṟṟa vēṭkikkum aṭiyākki iṭiviṭṭu nikaḻātu oru taṉmittāki nili peṟum vēṭki eṉap poruḷ varintār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
1.	taṉiyiṉāl - piṟar yārum illāta taṉimikkaṇ.
2.	nīṅkutalum vēṇṭumaṉṟē eṉṟiruttal poruttam.
1.	āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ulakattiluḷḷa talimakkaḷiyalpellām uṇarnturikkiṉṟārātaliṉ, muṉṉiliyākkal mutaliya ivi talimakkaḷ pāl nikaḻtal perumpāṉmiyeṉavum ivi nikaḻātu talimakkaḷiruvarum colliya nukarcci vallē peṟutal ciṟupāṉmiyeṉavum koḷka eṉa iḷampūraṇar tarum viḷakkam mikavum ēṟpuṭiyatākum.
1.	muṉṉiliyākkal mutalāka iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭavi iyaṟkip puṇarccik kaṇ nikaḻvaṉa eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar karuttu, iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ piritalum pirital nimittamumāy attuṟippaṭuvaṉa ellān tokuttut talivaṟkuriyaṉavāka innūṟpāvil kūṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. ikkaruttukku ēṟpac coṟkaḷi nalintum valintum ic cūttirattiṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uri cūttirak kiṭakki muṟikku ēṟpuṭiya taṉṟām. innūṟpāvukkum iḷampūraṇar tarum karuttum poruḷ viḷakkamumē tolkāppiyaṉār karuttiṉip pulappaṭuttum muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa,
1.	nikaḻum eṉpataṟkup payiṉṟuvarum eṉa muṉṉarp poruḷ varintamiyiṉ apporuḷi niṉivukūrum muṟiyil, payiṉṟu eṉavē ataṉi vitappuk kiḷaviyāka eṭutturittār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
1.	iṅṅaṉam kūṟupavar iḷampūraṇar.
iḷampūraṇar karuttiṉi maṟuttaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṭutturikkum kāraṇaṅkaḷ poruttamaṟṟaṉa. vēṭki mutalākac cākkāṭu īṟākac kūṟappaṭṭavi kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuc ciṟappuṭi marapiṉavi eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār teḷivākak kuṟittuḷḷār. avatti eṉpaṉa nallūḻiṉ āṇiyāl ōriṭattu etirppaṭṭa otta aṉpiṉarākiya oruvaṉ orutti eṉṉum iruvarkkum potup paṭa nikaḻum uṇarvu nilikaḷām. puṇarccikkuk kāraṇamāy nikaḻum avvuṇarvu nilikaḷi iyaṟkippuṇarccik kaṇ muṉṉarkkūṟutalē muṟi. iṅkuc collappaṭṭa uṇarvu nilikaḷiṉ veḷippāṭē 
muṉpakkattoṭarcci
piṉṉar meyppāṭṭiyalil viritturikkappaṭum akaṉintiṇi paṟṟiya meyppāṭukaḷākum. vēṭki mutaṟ cākkāṭīṟākac collappaṭṭa ivvuṇarvu nilikaḷ kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuc ciṟantaṉa eṉa iṅkuk kuṟittatallatu ivaṟṟuṭ cākkāṭu eṉṉum avatti (nili) eytiya piṉṉarē meyyuṟu puṇarcci naṭakkum eṉpatu vēṭkiyoṭu taliyuḷḷutal eṉa varum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvilō ataṟku iḷampūraṇar varintuḷḷa uriyilōyāṇṭum kūṟappaṭavilli. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil etirppaṭṭa kātalar, iruvarum tam kaṇṇiṉāṉ varum kuṟippaḷavil niṉṟu iṉmukamum iṉcollum iṉṟip pacuppōla ūminiliyiṟ kūṭiṉār eṉṟal makkaḷ nutaliya akaṉintiṇi yoḻukalāṟṟukku ēṟpuṭiyataṉṟām. eṉavē iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ taliviyi muṉṉiliyākkal colvaḻippaṭuttal mutalākac cila coṟkaḷip paṭikāy muṟimiyāṉ nikaḻttit taliviyik kūṭiṉāṉ eṉṟal avaṉatu talimikku ēṟpuṭiyatēyākum. iṅṅaṉam iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ uḷḷappuṇarcci nikaḻntu meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻvataṟku muṉ taliviyiṉpāl nikaḻvataṟkuriya meyppāṭukaḷiyē pukumukam purital mutal irukiyum eṭuttal īṟāka meyppāṭṭiyalil āciriyar viritturikkiṉṟār. talivaṉ nayappum pirivaccamum vaṉpuṟiyum nikaḻttip pirital iyaṟkip puṇarcciyoṭu toṭarpuṭiyatāy uṭaṉ nikaḻvatu eṉpatu, colliya nukarcci vallēpeṟṟuḻit tīrāt tēṟṟam uḷappaṭat tokiip pērācciṟappiṉ irunāṉku kiḷavuyum eṉa aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. eṉavē innūṟpāvukku iḷampūraṇar kūṟum karuttum viḷakkamumē tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēṟpuṭiyavātal naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
1.	poypārāṭṭal -- poyyāka oru kāraṇam paṭittuk koṇṭu talimakaḷip pārāṭṭal.
2.	iṭampeṟutalāvatu, talimakaḷip pārāṭṭumukamāka avaḷatu aṇimikkaṇ neruṅkutal.
1.	niṟpavi niṉii nikaḻpaḷi yurippiṉum eṉpataṉi niṟpavi niṉi iyurittal, nikaḻpaviyurittal eṉa iraṇṭākap pakutturippar iḷampūraṇar.
1.	iccūttirattuḷ talivaṉ kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟum aṭaṅkaṟkuk kūṟṟu variyaṟi kūṟārāyiṉār āciriyar eṉpatu karuttu.
1.	ivvāṟu irantu piṉṉiṟṟalum maṭalēṟuval eṉṟalum akattiṇikkum puṟattiṇikkum potuvāmāṟu paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum eṉa aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvāl viḷaṅkum.
2.	vitika neṟiyil vaḻuvā toḻukum maṟiyōṉ oruvaṉ pāl an neṟiyiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvit tavaṟu ceyta maṟiyōṉ taṉ tavaṟuṇarntu kalakkamaṭital pōlavum, uṇaviṉpāl vitta peruvēṭkiyāl taṉ muṉ vikkappaṭṭa cūṭāṉa uṇavi virintu uṭkoṇṭu vāyum vayiṟum ventu puṇṇuṟṟār pōlavum neñcattuṟuti yiṉiyum niṟiyiṉiyum iḻantu kalakkamaṭintu eṉpatu ivvurit toṭariṉ poruḷākum.
3.	taṉ tōḻiyar kūṭṭattuḷ varuvāṉāyiṉ nam kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭu viṭumē eṉṟa accamum, vantāl avaṉi mīṇṭum orumuṟi kaṇṭu makiḻalāmē eṉṟa kātalum talimakaḷ uḷḷattē tōṉṟum.
1.	aṉantar - mayakkam.
2.	maṟipulappaṭutal - kaḷavu veḷippaṭutal.
3.	itaṉiṉūuṅku - itaṟkumēl.
4.	payiṟal - palamuṟi tīṇṭip paḻakutal.
5.	poyceyyā niṟṟalāvatu, viḷiyāṭṭāka uriyāṭi aḷavaḷāvutal.
6.	citital - muṉṉirunta oppaṉi kalital.
7.	poḻutu nīṇṭataṟku iraṅkutalāvatu, talimakaḷi aṇiyappeṟātu kālam vaṟitē kaḻintamikku varuntutal.
1.	kūṭutal uṟutal - talivaṉ taṉṉik kūṭutaṟku iciyum muṟiyil talimakaḷ taṉatu nāṇam nīṅkap peṟutal.
2.	colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻi eṉpataṉic colliya nukarcci vallē peṟutal eṉap peyarppaṭutti eṇṇutal āciriyar karuttu eṉpatām.
3.	tīrāta tēṟṟam - tīrāmikku (viṭṭu nīṅkāmikku)k kāraṇamākiya tēṟṟam; eṉṟatu ekkālattum piriyāmikkuk kāraṇamākiya cūḷ (capatam) ceytal. iṅṅaṉam teyvattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟu cūḷuṟutal muṉtēṟṟu eṉappaṭum.
4.	muṉ teḷivakappaṭuttapiṉ eṉṟatu, muṉṉiliyākkal (kaḷaviyal 10) eṉṟa cūttirattil collappaṭṭa teḷivakappaṭuttal nikaḻntapiṉ eṉpatām.
5.	pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉpataṟkup pāṅkaṉatu uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟut talivaṉ talimakaḷik kūṭum kūṭṭam eṉa viḷakkam kūṟutalum poruntum.
1.	tāmē kūṭum iṭantalippāṭum pāṅkaṉār kuṟitalippeyyum iṭan talippāṭum eṉa iṭintalippāṭu iraṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttātaliṉ pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattiṉ piṉ nikaḻum iṭantalippāṭṭiṉum eṉavarum ivvurit toṭarkkup pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamākiya iṭantalippāṭṭiṉum eṉap poruḷ koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
1.	itu eṉṉum cuṭṭu, pirintavaḻik kalaṅkali uṇarttiyatu. pirinta vaḻik kalaṅkal eṉpatu mutalāka iṉik kūṟappaṭuvaṉa pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṟ kuriyaṉa eṉpatām.
1.	ulakattu niliniṟkum naṟpaṇpukaḷi niṟpavi eṉavum, talivaṉatu neñciṉkaṇ nikaḻum varuttaṅkaḷi nikaḻpavi eṉavum koṇṭu, niṟpavi niṉintu kaḻaṟiyurittalip pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟākavum, ataṉi etirmaṟuttut taṉ neñciṉ nikaḻpavi urittalit talivaṉ kūṟṟāvum, iruvēṟu tuṟikaḷākap pakuttu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri varintuḷḷār. niṟpavi eṉṟatu illaṟa viṉiyi eṉak koḷvar iḷampūraṇar. niṟpavi niṉii, eṉpataṉip pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟākap pakutturikkuṅkāl, niṟpavi niṉiik kaḻaṟiyurippiṉum eṉak kaḻaṟi eṉṉum oru col varuvitturikkappaṭṭatu. niṉii--niṉippittu; piṟaviṉi; talivaṉ niṉikkumāṟu ceyvittu eṉpatu.
2.	kaḻiyuvaki - mikka makiḻcci.
3.	ikkūṭṭam eṉṟatu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉi.
1.	ittuṇiyum - kuṟṟaṅkāṭṭiyavāyil peṭpiṉum eṉavarum ivvaḷavum.
1.	peṭṭavāyil - taliviyāṟ pēṇappaṭṭavaḷāyt taṉakku (talimakaṉukku) vāyilāntaṉmiyuṭiya tōḻi. vāyilip peṟutalāvatu talimakaḷatu nōkkākiya kuṟippiṉāl tōḻiyi vāyilāka aṟital.
2.	avaṭku avaḷ - taliviyākiya avaṭkut tōḻiyākiya avaḷ.
3.	avaḷi - attōḻiyi.
4.	talivaṉ taṉakkuṭ kūṟikkoṇṭatāka iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa kuṟuntoki 222 ām pāṭal, iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal oṉpatām cūttiravuriyil uṭaṉpaṭṭu vilakkal eṉṉun tōḻi kūṟṟukku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ippāṭalit tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟākat koḷḷutal taliviyiṉ arumikkum avaṭku ētum varumō eṉṟu añcum tōḻiyiṉ uṭkōḷukkum poruntātu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar maṟuttavāṟu.

3.	meyyuṟa - meyyeṉṟu uṇara. kūṟal - talivaṉ kūṟal, avaḷ eṉṟatu taliviyi.

1.	pāṅkar nimittam eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, palavaki maṇattiṟ pāṅkarāyiṉār tuṇiyākumiṭam eṉap poruḷurippar iḷampūraṇar. īṇṭu nimittam eṉpatu nimittamākak kūṭum kūṭṭattiṟku ākiyatu karuviyākupeyar. pāṅkarāṟ kūṭum kūṭṭam paṉṉiraṇṭāvaṉa : eṇvaki maṇattuḷ piramam, piracāpattiyam, āriṭam, teyvam eṉṉum muṉṉiya nāṉkum, kantiruvap pakutiyākiya kaḷavu, uṭaṉpōkku, kaṟpiṉ pakutiyākiya iṟkiḻatti, kāmak kiḻatti, kātaṟparatti eṉpaṉa aintum, acuram, irākkatam, picācam eṉṉum piṉṉiya mūṉṟum eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
īṇṭu nimittam eṉṉum col, kāraṇam eṉṟa poruḷilaṉṟik kāriyam eṉṟa poruḷil āḷappaṭāmiyāṉum, otta kāmamākik karupporuḷoṭum puṇarnta kantaruvaneṟi iṭavakiyāl aintuvakippaṭum eṉa iḷampūraṇar kuṟitta aintaṉuḷ kaḷavum uṭaṉpōkkum nīṅkalāka iṟkiḻatti, kāmakkiḻatti, kātaṟ, paratti eṉṉum mūṉṟum kaṟpiyalilaṉṟik kaḷaviyalil iṭampeṟāmiyāṉum, muṉṉiya mūṉṟu, piṉṉar nāṉku, mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōrmēṉa ainnilam eṉa iṅkup pakutturikkappaṭṭa paṉṉiraṇṭum itaṟku muṉṉar ivvāṟu niraṟpaṭuttu eṇṇum niliyil oru tokutiyākat tol kāppiyaṉārāṟ kūṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, iṟkiḻatti, kāmakkiḻatti, kātaṟparatti paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ ivviyalil iṭampeṟāmiyāṉum innūṟpākkaḷ nāṉkaṟkum iḷampūraṇar kūṟum poruḷ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēṟpuṭiyatāka amiyavilli.
1.	innūṟpāvil uḷḷa pāṅkaṉ eṉṉuñ col akaṉintiṇiyil varum pāṅkaṉik kuṟiyātu, ulakiyaliṟ kāḷiyaroṭu kaṉṉiyari maṇañceytu vittaṟkut tuṇiyāyiṉārik kuṟittu niṉṟatu eṉpatum, īṇṭup pāṅkaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṉum iruvaruṭiya kōttiraṅkaḷiyum naṉkuṇarntu naṭuniṉṟu tirumaṇatti naṭattivikkum pārppāṉ eṉpatum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttākum.
1.	pāṅkaṉ nimittam -- pāṅkaṉ kaṇṇavākiya nimittam eṉa viriyum. nimittam -- kāraṇam. kāraṇam paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉavē avaṟṟiṉ kāriyamum paṉṉiraṇṭām eṉak koṇṭu, eṇvaki maṇattuḷ oṉṟākiya kantaruvatti mulli, kuṟiñci, pāli, marutam, neytal eṉa aintākki avvintuṭaṉ piramaṉ mutaliya ēṉiya ēḻiṉiyum kūṭṭip paṉṉiraṇṭāka viritturittār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
2.	kantaruva maṇattiṉ pakuppāka iṅkuc collappaṭṭa aintiṇi maṇaṅkaḷum pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vakiyāl mutal karu uripporuḷ variyaṟi paṟṟi muṟi ciṟantu varutalum cuṭṭiyoruvarp peyarkoḷap peṟāmiyum ākiya iyalpuṭiya aṉpiṉ aintiṇi pālaṉṟi avvannilattiyalpālum piṟa moḻiyāḷar vāḻviyal vēṟupāṭukaḷālum cuṭṭiyoruvarp peyar koṭuttum ulakiyaliṟ palarum aṟiya nikaḻkiṉṟa maṇaṅkaḷām eṉpatum ivi akappuṟam eṉappaṭum eṉpatum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttākum.
pāṅkaṉimittam paṉṉiraṇṭeṉpa eṉavarum innūṟpā kaḷaviyalil viritturikkappaṭum aṉpiṉ aintiṇik kaḷavoḻukkattil oruvaṉum oruttiyum kūṭi aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiyavaṟṟik kuṟippataṉṟi ulakiyaliṟ palarum aṟiya nikaḻum kāriyaṅkaḷākiya tirumaṇa vakikaḷik kuṟippateṉṟum ippaṉṉiraṇṭum toṉmi (purāṇam), tōl (poruṭṭoṭar niliyākiya kāppiyam) ākiya vaṉappiṉuḷ varuvaṉaveṉṟum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar; karaṇamoṭu puṇara makaṭkoṭikkuriyōr koṭuppa maṇamakaṉ koḷvatākiya maṇa muṟiyiṉik kuṟittalē itaṉ karuttāyiṉ innūṟpā kaḷaviyalil iṭam peṟutaṟkilli. itu kaṟpiyalil iṭam peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum āriyarkaḷatu oḻukalāṟākiya eṇvaki maṇattip paṟṟiya viḷakkattiṟkut tamiḻiyal vaḻakkamē nutaliya ikkaḷaviyal iṭamākāmiyum iṅkuk karutattakuvatām.
1.	muṉṉiya mūṉṟu eṉpaṉa, vētattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa piramam mutalākavuḷḷa eṭṭu maṇaṅkaḷuḷ kālavakiyāṟ piṟ kūṟappaṭuvaṉavākiya acuram irākkatam picācam eṉṉum mūṉṟum eṉavum, piṉṉar nāṉku eṉpaṉa kālavakiyāl muṟkūṟappaṭṭaṉavākiya piramam piracāpattiyam āriṭam teyvam eṉṉum nāṉkum eṉavum, iṅku muṉ, piṉ eṉpaṉa iṭa vakiyiṉik kuṟittaṉa eṉavum koḷvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
2.	mēṟkuṟitta akaṉ aintiṇiyiṉiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu porunti varum kantaruva neṟiyāṉatu mulli kuṟiñci pāli marutam neytal eṉṉum aimpulattu vāḻvār maṇamāyc cuṭṭiyoruvarp peyar peṟṟuk kaṟpiyalākiya il vāḻkkiyum peṟṟuc ceyyuḷuṭ pāṭappeṟum eṉa innūṟpāvukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tarum viḷakkam, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku vēṟupaṭṭatāyiṉum ulakiyalōṭum piṟkālattuk tōṉṟiya kāppiyaṅkaḷākiya ceyyuḷ neṟiyōṭum porunti varutaliṉ it tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ poruḷoṭu toṭarpuṭiyatākak koḷḷattakuvatēyākum. innūṟpāvukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟum ipporuḷ iḷampūraṇar kālattilēyē kūṟappeṟṟu vanta toṉmiyuṭiyateṉpatu, ainnilam eṉpataṉi mulli kuṟiñci mutalāyiṉa eṉṟār uḷarāleṉiṉ mutaloṭu puṇarnta eṉpataṉāl nilam peṟumātalāṉ nilam eṉpataṟku vēṟuporuḷ urittal vēṇṭum eṉka eṉa varum iḷampūraṇar urippakutiyāṟ pulaṉām. 
muṉpakkattoṭarcci
ivvuriyiṉik kūrntuṇarnta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, mutaloṭu puṇarnta eṉpataṟku mēṟ kūṟiya akaṉ aintiṇiyum mutalāka avaṟṟoṭu poruntivarum eṉap poruḷ varintuḷḷami kāṇalām. eṉavē innūṟpāvukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar varintuḷḷa ivvuri iḷampūraṇar uriyuṭaṉ avar kālattil vaḻaṅkiya paḻiya uriyiṉiyum aṭiyoṟṟiyeḻutappeṟṟa teṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum.
1.	tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉ ainnilam eṉpataṟku mulli, kuṟiñci mutalāyiṉa eṉa iḷampūraṇar kālattil oru cārāciriyar poruḷ kūṟivantaṉar eṉpatu ainnilam eṉpataṉi mulli kuṟiñci mutalāyiṉa eṉṟār uḷarāleṉiṉ; mutaloṭu puṇarnta eṉpataṉāl nilam peṟumātalāṉ nilam eṉpataṟku vēṟu poruḷ urittal vēṇṭum eṉka eṉavarum iḷampūraṇar urippakutiyāṟ pulaṉām. īṇṭu ainnilam eṉṟatu aintu nilameṉṟa poruḷilaṉṟi aintu iṭam eṉṟa poruḷilēyēyātaliṉ, kantaruva neṟiyiṉi yotta kaḷavu aintiṇikaḷi iṭamākap peṟum eṉpār uriyum ēṟpuṭiyatēyākum.
1.	pukāṉ eṉpatu, muṟṟuvāypāṭṭāṉ vanta viṉiyoccam eṉa ivvuri yāciriyar kūṟutalāl viṉiyeccamē viṉimuṟṟuvāyppāṭṭāy tirintu varum eṉpatu ivar karutteṉat terikiṟatu.
2.	talivaṉ tāṉ pukutaṟkut takutiyillāta kālattukkaṇ akampukkut taliviyi etirppaṭṭāṉ eṉṟal avaṉatu talimikku ēṟṟataṉṟām. pukā akkāli eṉpataṟku uṇavuṇṇuṅkāli eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiya poruḷē āciriyar karuttukku ēṟpuṭiyatākum. pukāa--uṇavu. puṉattuḷāṉ entikkup pukāvuyttuk koṭuppatō (mullikkali 8) eṉpatu kalittoki, pakāaviruttu -- nīkka voṇṇātaviruntu.

1.	taṉimiyiṟ politalāvatu, piṟaraṟiyāvāṟu otta aṉpiṉarākiya iruvarum taṉittuḷḷa taṉimi niliyil nikaḻtalāl polivuṭiyatu kāmak kūṭṭamākiya kaḷavātaliṉ, talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvarum piṟari vāyilākak koḷḷātu tāme oruvarkkoruvar tamatu vēṭkiyip pulappaṭuttaṟkum uriyar eṉpatām.
1.	īṇṭuk kāmakkūṭṭam eṉpaṉa iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum, iṭantalippāṭum. taṉimiyiṟ politalāvatu, tām kūṭutaṟkut tuṇiyāyiṉār iṉṉār eṉak karutum vakiyil tammik kūṭṭuvippār oruvarumiṉṟi nāmē kūṭiṉōm eṉṉum taṉimi yuṇarvuṭaṉ tammiruvar aḷavilēyē polivu peṟṟu nikaḻtaliṉ avviruvarum tam karuttiṉip pulappaṭuttum niliyil oruvarkkoruvar tūtākit tammuṭ kūṭutalum urittu eṉpatām. ivvāṟu pāṅkaṉum tōḻiyum nimittamāka aṉṟi ivvāṟu tāmē tūtātal meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ pukumukam purital mutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭu meyppāṭukaḷāṉum nikaḻum. ikkūṭṭattiṉ piṉṉart talivaṉ taliviyi varinteytalum uṇṭu eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.

	cuṭṭutal -- kūṟṟāl aṉṟik kuṟippāṟ pulappaṭuttal.
1.	iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil etirppaṭṭu uḷḷappuṇarcciyaḷavil niṉṟa talivaṉ talivi iruvarkkum meyyuṟu puṇarcci tōḻiyiṉāl muṭiyum iṭaṉum uṇṭu.
2.	talimakaḷoṭu uḷḷap puṇarcci peṟātāṉ tōḻiyiṉ tuṇi peṟṟu avaḷik kūṭa muyalvatu peruntiṇiyākiya poruntā oḻukkamām eṉpatu karuttu.
3.	tōḻi cuṭṭiya iṭamum talivi cellutaṟkuriya iṭamātalāl taliviyuṭaṉ otta uṇarviṉaḷāki tōḻikkum kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi uriyatākum eṉkiṉṟatu innūṟpā.
1.	īṇṭut tuṇiyeṉṟatu pāṅkaṉāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarvataṉ muṉ pāṅkaṟku uṇarttavum peṟum eṉṉum ivvuri poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṉṟavilli. pāṅkaṟku uṇarttuvāṉāyiṉ atu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamākumēyaṉṟi iyaṟkip puṇarcciyākātu.
2.	iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉa mūṉṟu nāḷ eṉṉum ellikkuḷaṉṟik kaḷaviṟpuṇarcci kūṭṭuvippōr tuṇiyiṉṟi naṭavāteṉpatu karuttu.

vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa voḻukalum	(kaṟpiyal - 5)

1.	innūṟpā, aṟattoṭu nilikku ilakkaṇam kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. uyiriṉuñ ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē ... ... tāviṉaṉ moḻi kiḻavi kiḷappiṉum eṉat talivi kūṟṟākavum, muṉṉiliyaṟaṉeṉappaṭutal eṉṟiruvakip puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuppiṉum eṉat tōḻi kūṟṟākavum aṟattoṭu niliyiṉi yeṭutturitta tolkāppiyar īṇṭu mīṇṭum tuṇic cuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatākum eṉa ataṉiyē kūṟiṉāreṉṟal kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟattiṟku iṭaṉātal kāṇka.
innūṟpāvil taliviyāṟ cuṭṭappaṭun tuṇiyeṉṟatu taliviyuṭaṉ oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻiyiyē eṉpatum tāyāvāḷ ceviliyē eṉpatum innūṟpāvālum aṭuttuvarum nūṟpāvālum naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
2.	pāṅkaṟku uṟṟaturitta piṉṉart talivaṉ uriyāmiyum peṟṟām eṉa varum ivvurit toṭar caṅkavilakkiya ārāyccikkup peritum payaṉ taruvatākum. itaṉāl kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉ talivi yiruvarit tavira mūṉṟāmavarāka iṭiniṉṟu niṟivēṟṟum poṟuppu pāṅkiyoruttikkē yuṇmiyum, talivaṉatu aṉpiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa kaḷavoḻukkattiṟku icivaḷittaloṉṟē pāṅkaṉ ceyalātaliṉ atupaṟṟip piṉṉun talivaṉē viṉavuṅkaruttu pāṅkaṟku illiyeṉpatum, pāṅkaṉuḷḷattil akkaruttut tōṉṟi maṟi pulappaṭuttiyavāṟām eṉpatum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttātal naṉkupulaṉām.

1.	mati---aṟivu. uṭampaṭuttal-oṉṟupaṭuttu uṇartal. matiyuṭam paṭuttalāvatu, talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvar karuttiṉiyum tōḻi taṉkaruttiṉōṭu oṉṟupaṭutti uṇartal. ivvāṟu mūvar matiyiṉiyum oṉṟu paṭuttutaliṉ matiyuṭampaṭuttal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
2.	muṉ uṟa uṇartal eṉap pirittup poruḷ koḷḷāmal, muṉ uṟavu uṇartal eṉap pirittut talivaṉ talivi iruvarkkum iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ uṇṭākiya muntiya uṟaviṉit tōḻi uṇartal eṉap poruḷ urittāl varum kuṟṟam eṉṉiyeṉiṉ eṉa viṉavutal ittoṭariṉ karuttākum.

muṉpakkattoṭarcci
eṉavum avaḷ eṉavum varum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ muṟiyē talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum kuṟittu vaḻaṅkutalē marapākum eṉpatu taṉṉum avaḷum avaṉum cuṭṭi eṉṟāṅku varum tolkāppiyat toṭarkaḷāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. alla kuṟiyiṟ ceṉṟu mīḷvāḷ talimakaḷēyaṉṟit tōḻiyallaḷ eṉpatum iṅkuk karutat takuvatākum. talivaṉ ceyta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ iyaṟkic cūḻalkaḷāl mayaṅkiya nilimikkaṇ allakuṟippaṭutalum talimakaṭku urittu eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷākum.
1.	allakuṟippaṭutal eṉpatu kuṟiyallātataṉik kuṟiyeṉak koṇṭu ceṉṟu talivaṉi etirppaṭāmiyi uṇarttiyatu. kuṟimayaṅkiṉami talivaṉatu kuṟṟamaṉṟu.
2.	poḻutaṟintu vārāmiyiṉ iyaṟkiyil nikaḻntatē eṉpatu tōṉṟa amivu eṉṟār eṉṉum ivvuri viḷakkam poruttamuṭiyatēyākum.
1.	iṅku oruciṟi eṉṟatu, kaṟpuṭimakaḷirkkuriya maṉa moḻi meykaḷāṉākiya oḻukalāṟukaḷuḷ maṉattāṉ oḻukum oḻukkattiṉik kuṟittatu eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar karuttākum.
2.	innūṟpāviṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uri tolkāppiyar karuttiṉi aṟiyavoṭṭātu tamatu karuttiṉi ēṟṟiyurikkum vakiyil valintu eḻutappaṭṭatākum. kuṭaṅkoṇṭāḷ vīḻntāḷ eṉpuḻi kuṭamum vīḻntatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭumāṟu pōla, alla kuṟippaṭutalum avaṉvayiṉ urittu, eṉavē avaṉvayiṉ urittātalum tāṉē peṟappaṭum. ātaliṉ talivaṉum alla kuṟiyāl varuntuvaṉ eṉpataṟkut taṉiyoru nūṟpā kūṟa vēṇṭiyatilli.
iruvakik kuṟi piḻippākiyaviṭattut talivaṉatu kalakkam ivviyal 16-ām nūṟpāvil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷami iḷampūraṇar uriyāl pulaṉākum.
1.	maṟinta oḻukkam--kaḷavoḻukkam.
2.	mukūrttamum nāḷuntuṟantoḻukutal tītāmātaliṉ, kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukāniṉṟa talivaṉ mukūrttattiyum nāḷiṉiyum vaḻuvi oḻuka māṭṭāṉ eṉpatām.
1.	tuṟattaṟkuc ceyappaṭuporuḷ ōriyum nāḷumēyākavum, kūṭṭattit tuṟanta oḻukkam eṉak kūṭṭam eṉpataṉic ceyappaṭu poruḷāka varuvit turittal poruntātu.
ōriyi mukūrttam eṉak koṇṭa iḷampūraṇar uriyilum, ōriyum nāḷum tuṟanta oḻukkam eṉpataṟku mukūrttamum, nāḷum tuṟantu oḻukum oḻukkam eṉa ōriyum nāḷum tuṟattaṟkuc ceyappaṭuporuḷākak kūṟappaṭṭami kāṇalām.
maṟinta oḻukkattiṉkaṇ ōriyum nāḷumākiyavaṟṟit tuṟantoḻukum oḻukkam talivaṉukku illiyeṉpatē innūṟpāviṉ poruḷ. maṟinta oḻukkam eṉṟatu ulakattār aṟiyātu maṟintu oḻukum oḻukkamākiya kaḷaviṉi.
1.	āṟṟiṉatu arumi-naṭattaṟkiyalāta vaḻiyiṉ arumi. kaḷavoḻukkattil vaḻiyarumi paṟṟiya eṇṇamum accamum iṭiyūṟum talimakaṉukku illi eṉpatām.

1.	mutalvaṉ - kāraṇamāvāṉ: mutal - kāraṇam.
āyeṭṭeṉpa toḻil mutalniliyē	(col-vēṟṟumiyiyal - 21)
eṉpuḻi mutal eṉpatu kāraṇam eṉṟa poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷami iṅku oppu nōkkaṟpālataram.
1.	veḷippaṭa - kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa niliyil. paṭāmi veḷippaṭāta niliyil varital. āṟu maṇantukoḷḷum neṟi.
1.	kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa niliyil ivaḷ ivaṉukkuriyavaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉa ellārkkum pulaṉākiya niliyil avviruvaratu uṟavu kaṟpiyal eṉak karutappaṭumāyiṉum taliviyit talivaṉ ulakaṟiya maṇantu koḷvataṉ muṉṉum kaṟpiyalukkuriya ōtal paki tūtu kāraṇamākat talimakaḷip pirintu cellutal kūṭātu eṉa vaṟpuṟuttuvatu innūṟpāvākum.
2.	muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa ōtal paki tūtu eṉṉum mūṉṟum kāraṇamāka variyātu pirital illiyeṉavē tirumaṇattiṉ poruṭṭup poruḷvayiṟ piritalum nāṭṭiṉ pātukāppukku iṉṟiyamiyāta vēṟtaṟkuṟṟuḻip piritalum kāvaṟkup piritalum tali makaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻappeṟum eṉpatām.
1.	ōtal pakiyē tūtivi pirivē (akat-25) eṉa ippirivu mūṉṟum muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ ñāṅkaṟ kiḷanta mūṉṟu eṉṟār. ñāṅkar muṉpu. poruḷāka -- kāraṇamāka.
